### [102] [Flag Space, Matroidal Schur Algebras and the Steinberg Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20779)
*Carl Mautner*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过生成元和关系定义了扩展的拟阵Schur代数，在射影几何情形将其表示论与GL(n,q)的模Steinberg表示联系起来，在一般情形证明了该代数具有三角（Reedy）分解从而拟遗传，并给出了原始拟阵Schur代数的新描述及其与Brylawski-Varchenko旗空间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展作者与Tom Braden此前提出的拟阵Schur代数，以建立更广泛的代数结构，并揭示其与经典表示论（特别是GL(n,q)的Steinberg表示）以及组合几何（Brylawski-Varchenko旗空间）之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过生成元和关系定义扩展代数；在射影几何情形建立与GL(n,q)模Steinberg表示的联系；利用三角（Reedy）分解证明一般情形下代数的拟遗传性；重新描述原始拟阵Schur代数并阐明与Brylawski-Varchenko旗空间的关系。

Result: 在射影几何情形，扩展拟阵Schur代数的表示论与GL(n,q)的模Steinberg表示结构相关联；在一般情形，该代数具有三角/Reedy分解，因而是拟遗传的；获得了原始拟阵Schur代数的新刻画，并澄清了其与Brylawski-Varchenko旗空间之间的连接。

Conclusion: 扩展拟阵Schur代数是一类具有良好同调性质（拟遗传）的代数，它统一了拟阵组合、Schur代数表示论和有限线性群的模表示，为相关领域的进一步研究提供了新的代数框架。

Abstract: We define via generators and relations an extended version of the matroidal Schur algebras introduced in earlier work of the author with Tom Braden. In the case of projective geometries, we relate the representation theory of the resulting algebras to the structure of the modular Steinberg representation of $GL(n,q)$. In the general case we show that the extended matroidal Schur algebras have a triangular, or Reedy, decomposition and are thus quasi-hereditary. We also obtain a new description of the original matroidal Schur algebras and clarify the connection to the flag spaces of Brylawski--Varchenko.

</details>


### [103] [Protected corners and a trichotomy for Han's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20849)
*Marco Armenta*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Han猜想在tau-Hochschild框架下的分解问题，证明了保护角定理以限制幸存顶点上的Ext，解决了Liu-Morin扩展猜想，并建立了三个强连通顶点上Gap-A失效的三分法，其中全无限情形不可能，双无限情形被完全分类为相互哑铃结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Han猜想预测有限维代数若Hochschild同调最终消失则具有有限整体维数。在Cibils等人提出的tau-Hochschild框架中，该猜想被分解为持久性（Gap A）和存活性（Gap B）两部分，而Gap-A的失效即可构成反例。作者旨在通过保护角定理限制幸存顶点上的Ext，从而解决Liu-Morin扩展猜想，并对Gap-A失效情形进行系统分类。

Method: 采用tau-Hochschild同调框架，通过证明保护角定理来界定幸存顶点上的Ext群结构。利用三分法对三个强连通顶点上的Gap-A失效情形进行系统分类：证明全无限情形不可能存在，并将双无限情形完全分类为相互哑铃结构。

Result: 成功证明了保护角定理，将Liu-Morin扩展猜想的适用范围从单项式和特殊双列情形推广到更一般情形；建立了三个强连通顶点上Gap-A失效的三分法，排除了全无限情形，并完成了双无限情形的完全分类。

Conclusion: 本文在tau-Hochschild框架下推进了Han猜想的研究，通过保护角定理和三分法分类，为理解有限维代数的同调性质提供了新的理论工具，并显著推广了Liu-Morin扩展猜想的已知结果。

Abstract: Han's conjecture predicts that a finite-dimensional algebra with eventually vanishing Hochschild homology has finite global dimension. In the tau-Hochschild framework of Cibils, Lanzilotta, Marcos and Solotar, it splits into persistence (Gap A) and survival (Gap B), and a Gap-A failure is already a counterexample. We prove a protected corner theorem bounding Ext at a surviving vertex, settling the Liu-Morin extension conjecture beyond the monomial and special biserial cases. We then establish a trichotomy for Gap-A failures on three strongly connected vertices: the all-infinite case is impossible, and the two-infinite case is completely classified as a mutual dumbbell.

</details>


### [104] [Closed formulas for energy functions on tensor squares of higher-level perfect crystals in classical affine types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20934)
*Shaolong Han*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为经典仿射类型中所有七种类型的 level-l 完美晶体张量积 B_l ⊗ B_l 构造了显式闭式坐标公式的局部能量函数，通过单一有限层级的最大线性公式统一描述。能量函数无需组合 R-矩阵即可计算，并应用于 KMN 路径特征公式，得到权重模的显式正坐标路径和。主特化后，路径指数可重写为位置无关的相邻对统计量的加权和，与特化 Weyl-Kac 特征公式比较，导出一族 uniform 的 level-l Rogers-Ramanujan 型恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为经典仿射类型的 level-l 完美晶体张量积构造显式闭式坐标公式的局部能量函数，以便在不依赖组合 R-矩阵的情况下计算能量，并应用于路径特征公式和 Rogers-Ramanujan 型恒等式的推导。

Method: 通过单一有限层级最大线性公式，将能量函数表示为两种分支的最大值（在 A_n^{(1)} 中两分支重合为循环部分和最大值，其余六类中每分支为有限个显式分段线性表达式在带杠坐标下的最大值），直接在有限晶体上验证局部能量递归并推导等价递归形式。随后代入 KMN 路径特征公式，并在主特化后将路径指数重写为加权相邻对统计量之和。

Result: 得到了所有七种经典仿射类型 level-l 完美晶体张量积 B_l ⊗ B_l 的局部能量函数显式闭式坐标公式；导出了无需组合 R-矩阵的能量计算方法；给出了所有 level-l 可积最高权模的特征的显式正坐标路径和；导出了一族 uniform 的 level-l Rogers-Ramanujan 型恒等式，并在每个家族的低秩 level-2 情形展示了完整的相邻对度数矩阵及相应恒等式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了经典仿射类型中 level-l 完美晶体能量函数的统一显式公式，为路径特征计算和 Rogers-Ramanujan 型恒等式提供了系统框架，并通过具体低秩例子验证了结果的有效性。

Abstract: For every $l\geq1$, we construct explicit closed-form coordinate formulas for the local energy functions on the tensor products $B_l\otimes B_l$ of level-$l$ perfect crystals in classical affine types. A single finite-level max-linear formula covers all seven types: its two branches coincide in type $A_n^{(1)}$, yielding a cyclic maximum of partial sums, whereas in the remaining six types each branch is the maximum of finitely many explicit piecewise-linear expressions in barred coordinates, with type-dependent boundary data. We verify the defining local-energy recursion directly on the finite crystals and derive equivalent recursive forms, allowing the energy to be evaluated without applying the combinatorial $R$-matrix. Substitution into the KMN path character formula gives explicit positive coordinate path sums for the characters of all level-$l$ integrable highest weight modules. After principal specialization, the path exponent can be rewritten as a weighted sum of a position-independent adjacent-pair statistic; comparison with the specialized Weyl--Kac character formula yields a uniform family of level-$l$ Rogers--Ramanujan-type identities equating these sums with explicit infinite products. For a representative low-rank case at level two in each family, we display the complete adjacent-pair degree matrix and list the resulting identities.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [An obstruction and residual-completion theory for Newman--Janis deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20565)
*Reggie C. Pantig,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于Newman-Janis类黑洞种子变形的新框架，将N-J算法视为离壳映射而非解生成定理，定义了Newman-Janis阻碍张量来编码失败，并引入残差补全过程，通过Schwarzschild例子展示了框架应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Newman-Janis算法及其变体在生成旋转黑洞解时缺乏严格的失败诊断工具，传统上依赖猜测复化规则。本文旨在建立系统化的形式化框架，定量判断N-J类变形何时成功、何时需要修正、何时根本不可行。

Method: 将Newman-Janis算法处理为从静态种子到稳态轴对称试验几何的离壳映射；定义Newman-Janis阻碍张量（试验构型代入目标场方程后的残差）；将残差分解为动力学、几何/坐标允许性和源守恒三个通道；当阻碍非零时，引入残差补全过程，在旋转参数最低非零阶将问题转化为线性可解性条件（阻碍须在规范固定的补全算子像中，余核条件给出no-go判据）。

Result: 以Schwarzschild种子在真空GR中为例，采用非Kerr生成的ONJA型复化得到非Kerr旋转试验度规并计算其阻碍，得到$n_\star=2$、$\ell_\star=0$且同阶存在额外四极分量；通过最小偶宇称修正恢复了Kerr复化，消除了主导阶残差。

Conclusion: 该框架将寻找正确复化规则的问题替换为可计算的成功/补全/阻碍判据，为N-J类变形提供了系统化的诊断和修正工具。

Abstract: We formulate an obstruction and residual-completion theory for Newman-Janis-type deformations of black hole seeds, where Newman-Janis-type includes both the original complex-coordinate Newman-Janis algorithm and modified prescriptions such as non-complexification variants. The Newman-Janis algorithm is treated as an off-shell map from a static seed to a stationary-axisymmetric trial geometry, rather than as a solution-generating theorem. Its failure is encoded in a Newman-Janis obstruction tensor, defined as the residual obtained after substituting the trial configuration into the intended field equations. We decompose this residual into dynamical, geometrical/coordinate-admissibility, and source-preservation channels, separating field-equation failure from circularity, Boyer-Lindquist integrability, stress-tensor type, equation-of-state preservation, and matter-model realizability. When the obstruction is nonzero, residual completion asks whether a minimal correction of the metric and matter fields can cancel it. At leading nonzero order in the rotation parameter, this becomes a linear solvability problem: the obstruction must lie in the image of a gauge-fixed completion operator subject to boundary and source-sector constraints, while the cokernel condition gives a no-go criterion in the chosen ansatz class. We illustrate the framework with a Schwarzschild example in vacuum GR. Using an ONJA-type complexification different from the Kerr-generating one, we obtain a non-Kerr rotating trial metric and compute its obstruction. The example gives $n_\star=2$ and $\ell_\star=0$, with an additional quadrupolar component at the same order, and shows how the leading residual is removed by the minimal even-parity correction restoring the Kerr complexification. The framework replaces the search for the correct complexification rule with computable criteria for success, completion, or obstruction.

</details>


### [106] [Eikonal Quality-Factor Parameterization of Model-Conditional Static Black-Hole Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20573)
*Nikko John Leo S. Lobos,Emmanuel T. Rodulfo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用单个复准正则模频率参数化一族静态球对称黑洞的几何与模型条件依赖的热力学性质。在eikonal极限下，标量场的比率χ=ω_Rτ=ω_R/ω_I=2Q可消除整体质量标度。若χ在某一无量纲参数上是单射的，则可重建该参数和几何标度。对于RN族，χ确定u=q²但无法区分电荷符号。静态MOG度规在常耦合下恰好是RN形式，导致完整标量谱在多极和泛音上完全相同。两种赋值具有相同温度但不同的熵。Chebyshev和WKB-Padé计算量化了有限多极误差，测试的l=2 RN网格上最大eikonal比率误差为2.1×10⁻³。该构造是模型依赖的反演，而非理论选择器。应用于引力波观测需要超越测试场eikonal近似的张量、矢量和标量微扰扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何从单个准正则模频率信息中反推黑洞的几何结构和热力学性质，建立模型依赖的黑洞参数重建方法，并区分不同引力理论。

Method: 利用eikonal极限下质量标度不变的比率χ=ω_R/ω_I=2Q作为核心量，通过单射性条件重建几何参数；采用Chebyshev和WKB-Padé方法计算有限多极误差；通过RN族和MOG度规的映射关系检验方法的有效性。

Result: χ可确定RN族中的电荷参数u=q²但无法区分电荷符号；MOG度规与RN度规在标量谱上完全一致，导致热力学温度相同但熵不同；有限多极误差在l=2时最大为2.1×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 该方法仅能实现模型依赖的参数反演而非理论选择，无法通过标量eikonal准正则模区分RN黑洞和MOG黑洞；实际引力波应用需考虑张量、矢量和标量微扰扇区。

Abstract: We study how one complex quasinormal frequency can parameterize the geometry and model-conditional thermodynamics of a prescribed family of static, spherically symmetric black holes. For a minimally coupled massless test scalar in the eikonal limit, the ratio $χ=ω_Rτ=ω_R/ω_I=2Q$ removes the overall mass scale. If $χ$ is injective in one dimensionless parameter, that parameter and the geometric scale can be reconstructed within the chosen family. The normalized metric then fixes the horizon and Hawking temperature, whereas Wald entropy requires the gravitational action. For the RN-form family, $χ$ determines $u=q^2$ but not the sign of charge. The constant-coupling static MOG metric is exactly RN under $\mathcal M=(1+α)m_{\rm MOG}$ and $u=α/(1+α)$; consequently, the complete minimally coupled scalar spectrum is identical on mapped backgrounds for every multipole and overtone. The two assignments have equal temperature but different action-dependent entropy. Chebyshev and WKB--Padé calculations quantify the finite-multipole error, with a maximum eikonal ratio error of $2.1\times10^{-3}$ on the tested $l=2$ RN grid. The construction is therefore a model-dependent inverse, not a theory selector. Applying it to gravitational-wave observations requires the appropriate tensor, vector, and scalar perturbation sectors beyond the test-field eikonal approximation.

</details>


### [107] [Unstable de Sitter inflationary solution in sixth-order gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20575)
*Tuan Q. Do*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种包含六阶导数项（γ₁R□R 和 γ₂Rμν□Rμν）以及二次和三次曲率项的引力理论，发现该理论存在精确的德西特解，其稳定性由系数3γ₁+γ₂的符号决定，若该值为负则解不稳定。结果表明该六阶引力更适合描述早期宇宙暴涨，而其二阶极限总能给出稳定的德西特解，适用于晚期宇宙加速膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究包含六阶导数项（γ₁R□R 和 γ₂Rμν□Rμν）以及二次和三次曲率项的引力理论是否允许存在精确且稳定的德西特解，以评估其对宇宙不同演化阶段的适用性。

Method: 在均匀各向同性的FLRW度规背景下，基于欧拉-拉格朗日方程的有效方法推导六阶微分场方程；通过解析求解得到精确德西特解（等价于动力系统的不动点）；利用不等式条件（3γ₁+γ₂<0）判断稳定性，并通过数值计算验证。

Result: 得到了精确德西特解，γ₁和γ₂不贡献于解的值但影响稳定性；当3γ₁+γ₂<0时德西特解总是不稳定（为动力系统的排斥子）；二阶极限总能给出稳定德西特解。

Conclusion: 该六阶引力更适合描述早期宇宙暴涨阶段；其二阶极限适用于晚期宇宙加速膨胀。

Abstract: A sixth-order gravity, which involves not only two leading terms, $γ_1 R\Box R$ and $γ_2 R_{μν} \Box R^{μν}$, but also quadratic curvature terms along with cubic curvature ones, will be investigated in this paper to see if it admits an exact stable de Sitter solution. First, we will derive sixth-order differential field equations of this gravity under the homogeneous and isotropic Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker background spacetime, using the effective method based on the Euler-Lagrange equations. Then, we will analytically solve these field equations to figure out an exact de Sitter solution, which turns out to be equivalent to a fixed point of the corresponding dynamical system of the studied gravity. Interestingly, two coefficients, $γ_1$ and $γ_2$, do not contribute to the value of the obtained de Sitter solution. However, they affect on the stability of the de Sitter solution. In particular, if these two coefficients obey the following inequality, $3γ_1+γ_2 <0$, then the de Sitter solution will always be unstable. Furthermore, numerical calculations will be performed to verify that the de Sitter fixed point is indeed a repeller of the dynamical system once this inequality is satisfied. All these results indicate that the sixth-order gravity is more suitable for an inflationary phase of early universe. To be complete, two special limits of the studied gravity model, in which field equations are reduced to second-order and fourth-order, respectively, will be investigated. As expected, only the second-order limit can always give raise a stable de Sitter solution, compatible with an accelerated expansion of late-time universe.

</details>


### [108] [Black hole-neutron star binaries with high spins and large mass asymmetries: III. Properties of the ejected material and its electromagnetic signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20609)
*Konrad Topolski,Samuel D. Tootle,Paramvir Singh,Mattia Bulla,Luciano Rezzolla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟，系统研究了黑洞-中子星双星系统中动力学抛射物的几何、热力学和核合成性质，并通过辐射转移计算得到千新星光变曲线，与观测数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞-中子星双星系统是引力波和电磁波多信使天文学的重要源，但其抛射物性质及核合成产物的参数依赖性尚未被充分研究，尤其是大质量比和高自旋情况。

Method: 使用广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟大质量比、高自旋BHNS系统；监测潮汐瓦解和动力学质量抛射的能量角动量转移；利用核反应网络SkyNet计算r-过程元素丰度；使用辐射转移代码POSSIS计算千新星光变曲线并与观测数据比较。

Result: 揭示了高不等质量比、高自旋BHNS双星抛射物的几何、热力学和运动学特性；得到了r-过程元素丰度及其对前身星性质的依赖性；计算的光变曲线与AT2017gfo数据和S190814bv上限一致。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了对BHNS系统动力学抛射物的理解，验证了模拟结果与多信使观测的一致性，为未来引力波电磁对应体识别提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Compact binary systems consisting of a black hole (BH) and a neutron star (NS) are expected to provide detectable signals both in the gravitational wave and in the electromagnetic channels, thus representing excellent sources for multi-messenger astronomy. In this third paper in a series, we report on the geometric and thermodynamic properties of the purely dynamical ejecta component modeled in our general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamical simulations of BHNS systems over a comprehensive binary parameter space consisting of large mass ratio and high BH spin. The tidal disruption and dynamical mass ejection is studied by monitoring the transfer of energy and angular momentum to matter for the particular case of a highly unequal-mass binary with large BH spin, extending known results for low-mass-ratio, irrotational BHNS binaries. By varying the binary parameters, we investigate their influence on the geometric, thermodynamic, and kinematic properties of the ejected matter. Furthermore, we employ the nuclear-reaction network SkyNet to obtain the elemental abundances resulting from the r-process active in the ejecta and study how they depend on the properties of the progenitor objects. Finally, using the radiative-transfer code POSSIS, we compute the kilonova light-curves from the results of our simulations, discuss their bolometric and optical/near-infrared evolution, and compare them with the AT2017gfo data and upper-limits for S190814bv, finding overall consistency.

</details>


### [109] [Cosmological Inflation in f(R,T) Gravity with Chern-Simons Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20654)
*Maryam Shiravand,Saeed Fakhry,Mehrdad Farhoudi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R,T)引力线性框架下研究宇宙暴胀，引入由量子引力启发的Chern-Simons修正的暴胀子标量场。通过FLRW度规和慢滚近似推导修改后的Friedmann方程，采用三角函数和指数函数两种C-S耦合形式。计算标量谱指数、张量谱指数和张量标量比，模型预测与Planck 2018数据、Planck+BK15+BAO联合数据吻合良好。指数型C-S耦合对张量标量比施加了更强约束。对比分析表明，C-S修正能有效优化f(R,T)线性引力中张量谱指数和张量标量比的预测值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子引力理论的启发下，探索Chern-Simons修正对f(R,T)引力框架下宇宙暴胀模型的影响，以期获得与观测数据（特别是Planck 2018）更一致的暴胀可观测量预测，并进一步约束张量标量比等关键参数。

Method: 采用FLRW度规，在慢滚近似下推导修改后的Friedmann方程。选取三角函数和指数函数两种C-S耦合函数形式及对应的暴胀势。定义并计算暴胀慢滚参数，进而计算标量谱指数ns、张量谱指数nT和张量标量比r。通过约束自由参数使模型预测与Planck 2018等观测数据对比。此外，进行多组对比分析：无C-S修正模型、无线性f(R,T)模型、非线性f(R,T)模型。

Result: 模型预测与Planck 2018数据吻合良好；指数型C-S耦合函数对张量标量比r给出了更强的约束，并与Planck、BK15和BAO联合数据一致。C-S修正的引入有助于精炼f(R,T)线性引力中nT和r的数值预测。

Conclusion: 在f(R,T)线性引力框架下引入Chern-Simons修正能够有效改善暴胀模型对张量谱指数和张量标量比的预测精度，为理解早期宇宙暴胀动力学和量子引力效应提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological inflation within the framework of a linear form of f (R, T ) gravity that incorporates an inflaton scalar field augmented by a Chern-Simons correction induced by aspects of quantum gravity. Utilizing the FLRW metric, we derive the modified Friedmann equations under the slow-roll approximations. We consider two specific forms of the Chern-Simons coupling function, trigonometric and exponential, each paired with the choice of an inflaton potential. Then, we define the essential slow-roll parameters and acquire their required expressions in the proposed model. Subsequently, we compute the scalar spectral index, the tensor spectral index, and the tensor-to- scalar ratio. By adequately constraining the free parameters, the proposed model provides accurate predictions for these inflationary observables that are in good agreement with the Planck 2018 data. Furthermore, the model predictions for the Chern-Simons exponential coupling function impose a stronger limit on the value of the tensor-to-scalar ratio and also provide good agreement with the joint Planck, BK15 and BAO data. Meanwhile, for comparative analysis and better comparison of the model motivations, we also examine the model without the Chern-Simons correction, without the linear form of f (R, T ) gravity, and with a non-linear form of f (R, T ) gravity. As a general conclusion, the obtained findings indicate that the inclusion of the Chern-Simons correction approximately refines the values of the tensor spectral index and tensor-to-scalar ratio in the context of the linear form of f (R, T ) gravity.

</details>


### [110] [A product template bank to search for compact binary coalescences microlensed by isolated point mass lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20661)
*Sudhir Gholap,Sanjeev V. Dhurandhar,Shasvath J. Kapadia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对微引力透镜效应下引力波信号的模板库构建方法。在波光学效应导致信噪比损失高达30%的情况下，作者利用几何光学近似将微透镜信号建模为重叠信号，并通过放大因子的相位计算度量元素。在原有双星并合参数空间基础上增加了两个透镜参数（时间延迟和相对放大率），构建了约4000个模板的库，覆盖了拟合因子低于97%的透镜参数区域。该方法将未透镜模板库与透镜参数模板库的笛卡尔积作为目标模板库，将失配度降低了约一个数量级，并满足0.97的最小匹配准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模板搜索忽略引力波微透镜的波光学效应会导致高达30%的信噪比损失，因此需要构建专门针对微透镜引力波的模板库，以提高探测效率。

Method: 采用几何光学近似将微透镜信号建模为重叠信号，利用放大因子的相位计算度量元素；在原有CBC参数空间基础上增加两个透镜参数（时间延迟t_d和相对放大率μ_r）；通过识别拟合因子低于97%的透镜参数区域，构建包含约4000个模板的库，该库可表示为未透镜CBC模板库与透镜参数模板库的笛卡尔积。

Result: 所构建的乘积模板库相比未透镜模板库，失配度降低了约一个数量级（因子~10），且满足构建中设定的0.97最小匹配准则。

Conclusion: 该方法可以有效探测微透镜引力波信号，且模板库的笛卡尔积结构使得构建过程独立于未透镜CBC参数，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GW) from stellar mass compact binary coalescences (CBCs) that encounter a lens whose Schwarzschild radius is comparable to the GW wavelength, will be microlensed, leading to frequency-dependent modulations in the observed signal. Neglecting such wave-optics effects in standard templated searches for CBCs can result in signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) losses as large as 30%. Here, we present a prescription for constructing a template bank targeted at microlensed GWs. We consider CBCs microlensed by isolated point mass lenses, whose frequency-dependent amplification factor is known to have a closed-form analytical expression. However, to construct the template bank, it suffices to use the geometric optics approximation, effectively modeling the microlensed signal as overlapping signals. Moreover, given that matched filtering is mostly sensitive to the phase of the GW signal, we use the phase of the amplification factor to compute the metric elements. The usual CBC parameter space is appended by two extra parameters: (i) the time-delay ($t_d$) between images and (ii) the relative-magnification ($μ_r$). By identifying the region of the lensing parameter space where the fitting factor (FF) falls below 97% due to wave-optics effects, we construct a template bank consisting of ~4000 templates covering the selected lensing region, independent of the values of the unlensed CBCs' parameters. We show that the bank targeting microlensed CBCs is, to a very good approximation, a Cartesian product of the unlensed CBC bank and the bank spanning the lensing parameters. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by evaluating fitting factors using both the unlensed CBC bank and the product template bank, finding a reduction of up to a factor of ~10 in the mismatch, as well as consistency with the prescribed minimal match criterion of 0.97 used in the construction of the banks.

</details>


### [111] [A First-Order Gauge Approach to de Sitter General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20687)
*P. Salgado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种德西特相对论的一阶规范表述，其中伪半径l(x)被提升为时空场，将SO(4,1)局域破缺至SO(3,1)。l(x)作为几何补偿场，dl(x)为序参量。该理论修正了强等效原理：局域真空随物质分布变化而非固定不变，而广义相对论的自由度保持不变。通过Inonu-Wigner收缩，抽象生成元变为显式时空变换。广义Noether定理给出统一的能量-动量流，无物质时恒为零，导致局域真空能渐近消失且背景衰变为闵氏时空。独立变分得到耦合的德西特场方程，并代数证明其与坐标流形上的张量分裂等价。规范Bianchi恒等表明Λ(x)是时空挠率的内禀源，将其一致地识别为对称性破缺的局域序参量。宇宙学常数问题被重新表述而非数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学中宇宙学常数问题（即Λ的理论预期值与观测值之间的巨大差异）长期未解。作者试图通过将德西特相对论规范化为局域对称性破缺框架，将Λ重新解释为动力学场而非固定参数，从而为这一问题提供新的理论视角。

Method: 采用一阶规范表述，将德西特时空的伪半径l(x)提升为动力学场（几何补偿场），实现SO(4,1)→SO(3,1)的局域对称性破缺。利用Inonu-Wigner收缩连接抽象代数与显式时空变换，通过广义Noether定理导出统一流，并利用规范Bianchi恒等分析Λ(x)与时空挠率的关系。

Result: (1) 修正了强等效原理，局域真空随物质分布动态变化；(2) 无物质时能量-动量流恒为零，局域真空能渐近消失，背景衰变为闵氏时空；(3) 独立变分得到耦合的德西特场方程，并证明其与坐标流形张量分裂等价；(4) Λ(x)被识别为时空挠率的内禀源，即对称性破缺的局域序参量。

Conclusion: 宇宙学常数问题被重新表述为一个动力学问题：Λ(x)不再是一个固定常数而是动态场，其从早期宇宙高密度值衰变至当前观测值仍需未来研究。该工作为理解真空能与宇宙学常数提供了新的规范理论框架，但尚未给出具体数值解。

Abstract: We propose a first-order gauge formulation of de Sitter relativity in which the pseudo-radius l(x) is promoted to a spacetime field, locally breaking SO(4,1) to SO(3,1). Here, l(x) is a geometric compensator and dl(x) the associated order parameter. This modifies the Strong Equivalence Principle: the local vacuum follows the matter distribution instead of remaining fixed, while the propagating degrees of freedom of General Relativity are unchanged. At fixed l(x), the action is SO(3,1)-gauge invariant, but varying l(x) destroys covariance under the full translational sector of SO(4,1). Through the Inonu-Wigner contraction, abstract generators become explicit spacetime transformations, yielding nonlinear Killing vectors in both the macroscopic l tends to infinity and microscopic l tends to 0 limits. The generalized Noether theorem gives a unified energy-momentum current coupling the standard tensor to the proper conformal matter current. It vanishes identically without matter, causing the asymptotic extinction of local vacuum energy and the decay of the background to Minkowski spacetime. Independent variations yield the coupled de Sitter field equations, and we algebraically prove their equivalence to the tensorial splitting on the coordinate manifold. Gauge Bianchi identities further show that Lambda(x) is an intrinsic source of spacetime torsion, consistently identifying it with the local order parameter of the symmetry breaking. The cosmological constant problem is therefore recast, not numerically solved: deriving the decay of Lambda(x) from early-universe densities to its observed value remains future work.

</details>


### [112] [Gravitational Wave Birefringence in generalized Palatini Chern Simons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20689)
*Jose Perdiguero,Macarena Lagos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(R)引力与动力学Chern-Simons项耦合的模型中，引力波在宇称破缺时的宇宙学双折射现象，比较了度规和Palatini两种形式体系下的预测差异，并利用三种常见f(R)模型拟合宇宙学数据，发现双折射效应可被增强1%-2倍，且红移依赖呈多项式增长而非线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇称对称性破缺时引力波传播的振幅和相位偏振畸变（宇宙学双折射），特别是在f(R)引力与dCS项耦合的框架下，比较度规与Palatini两种形式体系对双折射现象的预测差异。

Method: 在f(R)引力模型中引入动力学Chern-Simons（dCS）项，分别在度规形式和Palatini形式下分析引力波传播方程；采用三种f(R)模型（Hu-Sawicki、指数引力、双曲引力）并拟合最新宇宙学数据，计算双折射效应的增强因子；将现有观测约束转化为dCS耦合参数界限。

Result: Palatini形式同时预测振幅双折射和速度双折射，度规形式仅预测振幅双折射；f(R)相互作用可抑制或增强双折射效应；三种模型中双折射相对f(R)=R情况增强1-10%（Palatini）或高达2倍（度规）；双折射效应随源红移呈多项式增长，不同于常见的线性距离依赖。

Conclusion: f(R)-dCS耦合模型为引力波双折射提供了丰富的现象学特征，形式体系的选择和f(R)函数形式对效应大小有显著影响，未来观测可据此检验修改引力理论。

Abstract: The cosmological propagation of gravitational waves (GWs) can exhibit amplitude and phase polarization distortions when parity symmetry is broken, a phenomenon known as cosmological birefringence. In this paper, we investigate the phenomenology of GW birefringence in a gravitational model $f(R)$ coupled to a dynamical Chern-Simons (dCS) term, analyzed in the metric and Palatini formalisms. At the background level, this model can lead to dynamical dark energy, while for GW propagation we find that the Palatini formalism predicts both amplitude and velocity birefringence, whereas the metric formalism predicts amplitude birefringence only. We also find that the birefringence effects can either be suppressed or enhanced by the $f(R)$ interactions, depending on the specific form of $f(R)$. Considering three common $f(R)$ models (Hu-Sawicki, Exponential, and Hyperbolic gravity) fit to recent cosmological data, we find that birefringence is enhanced relative to the $f(R) = R$ case, by $1-10\%$ in the Palatini formalism and up to a factor of 2 in the metric formalism. We also translate current GW birefringence constraints to bounds on the dCS coupling within our model. Finally, we show that in our model the birefringence effect grows polynomially with source redshift, in contrast to the linear-distance scaling commonly assumed in phenomenological models of GW birefringence in the current literature.

</details>


### [113] [Evolving Dark Energy from the Back-Reaction of Cosmological Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20715)
*Vincent Comeau*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证了宇宙不均匀性的反作用与纯宇宙学常数结合时，会产生一个状态方程随时间演化的有效暗能量。通过两种微扰方法分析发现，该时间依赖性在梯度展开法中不会退化为边界项，且在当前时期保持符号不变，不允许暗能量在phantom与非phantom行为之间交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇宙不均匀性的反作用能否导致暗能量状态方程随时间演化，以解释当前宇宙加速膨胀现象。

Method: 采用两种微扰方法：(1) 将不均匀性视为均匀背景上的小扰动（标量模至二次阶）；(2) 对不均匀性的空间导数进行梯度展开。在固定物质粒子体积上对Friedmann方程取平均，推导有效暗能量的状态方程。

Result: (1) 小扰动方法下，暗能量的时间依赖性退化为边界项，全局平均时可忽略；(2) 梯度展开方法下，同一时间依赖性不再退化为边界项，即使全局平均或空间曲率可忽略时仍显著；(3) 该时间依赖性在当前时期保持同号，导致暗能量始终为phantom或始终为非phantom，不允许交叉。

Conclusion: 不均匀性的反作用可以产生随时间演化的有效暗能量，但其行为受限于不能跨越phantom边界，为理解宇宙加速膨胀提供了理论机制，同时给出了对暗能量状态的约束。

Abstract: We argue that the back-reaction of inhomogeneities, when combined with a pure cosmological constant, produces an effective dark energy whose equation of state can evolve with time. Our analytical computation agrees with recent numerical simulations, which have shown that the back-reaction of inhomogeneities can impact dark energy around the present time. Here, its equation of state is derived from an average version of the Friedmann equations, for which the average is taken over a fixed volume of matter particles (not necessarily global). When treating the inhomogeneities as small perturbations around a homogeneous background, dark energy acquires a time dependence which reduces to a boundary term, at least at quadratic order in the scalar modes of the perturbations. Thus, it becomes negligible for a global average. On the other hand, when treating instead the spatial derivatives of the inhomogeneities perturbatively in a gradient expansion, dark energy gets the same time dependence, which no longer reduces to a boundary term. This time dependence can be significant even for a global average or when the constant spatial curvature induced by the inhomogeneities is negligible. It also keeps the same sign around the present time, thus causing dark energy to remain either always phantom or never phantom, not allowing for any crossing from one type of behaviour to the other.

</details>


### [114] [Thermodynamics of boosted Schwarzschild black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20746)
*Glenn Barnich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文以简洁方式推导了 boosted Schwarzschild 黑洞的平衡热力学，并证明其与相对论热力学的预期一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证 boosted Schwarzschild 黑洞的热力学性质是否符合相对论热力学的理论框架。

Method: 采用平衡热力学方法，对 boosted Schwarzschild 黑洞进行热力学量的推导与计算。

Result: 推导结果与相对论热力学的预期结果相符。

Conclusion: Boosted Schwarzschild 黑洞的平衡热力学与相对论热力学一致，为该类黑洞的热力学描述提供了理论支持。

Abstract: The equilibrium thermodynamics of boosted Schwarzschild black holes is worked out in a concise way and shown to agree with expectations from relativistic thermodynamics.

</details>


### [115] [Multi-Spin Perturbations, Thermodynamics, and Observational Signatures of Reissner-Nordstrom Black Holes in Bumblebee Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20839)
*Jayasri Choudhury,Yenshembam Priyobarta Singh,Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Telem Ibungochouba Singh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在蜂巢引力框架下系统研究了带电荷的Reissner-Nordström黑洞的动力学和热力学性质，分析了自发洛伦兹对称性破缺参数L和黑洞电荷Q对拟正规模、灰体因子、霍金温度、熵及热容等物理量的影响，发现二阶相变受电荷主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹对称性破缺对带电黑洞动力学稳定性、热演化及相结构的影响，为Lorentz破坏理论提供黑洞物理层面的理论见解。

Method: 运用Newman-Penrose形式下的统一Teukolsky主方程分析自旋s=0,1/2,1,3/2,2的无质量场扰动；采用Padé改进的6阶WKB近似和渐近迭代法计算拟正规模频率；评估灰体因子；分析霍金温度、含对数修正的熵及热容，确定二阶相变临界半径。

Result: 洛伦兹破缺参数L和电荷Q显著改变了时空的阻尼振荡和波传播特性；给出了LISA、Virgo、LIGO等探测器可观测的黑洞质量范围；热力学分析显示存在二阶相变，临界半径主要由电荷决定。

Conclusion: 综合动力学与热力学分析揭示了Lorentz破坏如何影响带电黑洞的物理稳定性、热演化和相结构，为后续引力波观测和量子引力研究提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a comprehensive investigation into the dynamical and thermodynamic properties of the charged Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole (BH) in the bumblebee gravity framework, where spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking (LSB) occurs. To analyze the dynamical behavior, we apply the unified Teukolsky master equation within the Newman-Penrose formalism to evaluate massless field perturbations of arbitrary spins ($s=0, 1/2, 1, 3/2, 2$). By deriving the corresponding effective potentials, we compute the quasinormal modes (QNMs) frequencies using the Padé-improved 6th-order WKB approximation and the Asymptotic Iteration Method (AIM) and evaluate the greybody factors for all perturbing fields, demonstrating how the LSB parameter $L$ and the BH charge $Q$ modify the spacetime's damped oscillations and wave propagation. We further assess the observational prospects of these QNMs by determining the black-hole mass ranges accessible to current and future gravitational-wave detectors, including LISA, Virgo, and LIGO. Moreover, we investigate the modified thermodynamic structure, calculating the Hawking temperature, entropy with logarithmic thermal corrections and heat capacity. Our thermodynamic analysis reveals a second-order phase transition whose critical radius is heavily governed by the background charge. These combined findings provide valuable theoretical insights into how Lorentz violation affects the physical stability, thermal evolution, and phase structure of charged BHs.

</details>


### [116] [Static spheres in black hole spacetimes: pairing, energy conditions, and an upper bound on the innermost radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20850)
*Yong Song*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般静态球对称渐近平直黑洞时空中静态球的存在性、与能量条件的关系及径向边界。通过分析由质量和径向压力函数构造的径向函数全局行为，证明静态球必然要求负径向压力（张力），且非退化静态球总是成对出现：内层不稳定球和外层稳定球。在弱能量条件(WEC)始终成立下，内层静态球表现为强能量条件(SEC)不等式破坏($\rho+p+2p_T<0$)，外层恢复SEC不等式($\rho+p+2p_T>0$)，退化边缘情况满足$\rho+p+2p_T=0$。针对最内层静态球且假设WEC成立而SEC在视界与该球之间均匀破坏，推导出其半径的严格上界公式。这些结果建立了能量条件与静态球存在性之间的直接解析联系，为拥有此类轨道的黑洞提供了物质环境的定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究静态球（黑洞周围物质可能形成稳定或非稳定的球形轨道）与能量条件之间的内在联系，建立严格的解析约束，从而为检验广义相对论和修正引力理论中的黑洞解以及解释相关天文观测提供理论工具。

Method: 通过构造和分析由质量和径向压力函数定义的径向函数的全局行为，运用解析方法证明静态球的存在性、成对性及稳定性特征，并结合弱能量条件(WEC)和强能量条件(SEC)的假设推导出静态球半径的严格上界。

Result: (1) 静态球必然要求负径向压力；(2) 非退化静态球成对出现（内不稳定、外稳定）；(3) WEC成立时，内层球破坏SEC，外层球恢复SEC，退化情况SEC等式成立；(4) 导出了最内层静态球半径的解析上界。

Conclusion: 建立了能量条件与静态球存在性之间的直接解析联系，提供了黑洞周围物质环境的定量约束，对检验黑洞解和解释天文观测具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the existence, relation to the energy conditions, and radial bounds of static spheres in general static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat black hole spacetimes. By analyzing the global behavior of a radial function constructed from the mass and radial pressure functions, we prove that a static sphere necessarily requires a negative radial pressure (tension). Furthermore, we show that non-degenerate static spheres must always appear in pairs: an inner unstable sphere and an outer stable one. Assuming the Weak Energy Condition (WEC) always holds, the inner static sphere is characterized by a violation of the strong energy condition (SEC) inequality $ρ+p+2p_T<0$, where $ρ$, $p$, and $p_T$ denote the energy density, radial pressure, and tangential pressure, respectively; the SEC inequality is restored ($ρ+p+2p_T> 0$) at the outer sphere; the degenerate marginal case satisfies $ρ+p+2p_T=0$. In addition, focusing on the innermost static sphere and assuming that the WEC holds while the SEC is uniformly violated between the event horizon and this sphere, we derive a rigorous upper bound on its radius,
  \[
  r^-_{\mathrm{sp}}\le \left[r_H^3+\frac{3r_H\bigl(1-8πr^2_Hρ(r_H)\bigr)}{8πκ}\right]^{1/3},
  \]
  where $r_H$ is the horizon radius, $ρ(r_H)$ the energy density at the horizon, and $κ$ characterizes the strength of the SEC violation. These results establish a direct, analytic link between the energy conditions and the existence of static spheres, and provide a quantitative constraint on the matter environment of black holes possessing such orbits. The findings have potential applications in testing black hole solutions in general relativity and modified theories of gravity, as well as in interpreting related astronomical observations.

</details>


### [117] [Graviton-induced which-path decoherence in matter-wave interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20867)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了物质波干涉中由线性化量子引力中的引力子追踪引起的路径退相干，发现真空退相干指数与引力子辐射数相关，且在当前物质波干涉仪中可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力效应在物质波干涉实验中是否会导致可观测的退相干，从而检验量子引力理论的实验可及性。

Method: 基于线性化量子引力框架，将引力子作为环境自由度进行追踪；利用引力子被物质源线性驱动的特性，精确求解分支依赖演化；通过初始引力子态的特征函数计算约化相干性。

Result: 真空退相干指数Γ_vac=(8/15)Gm²d⁴/(ħc⁵τ⁴)，取值范围10⁻⁸⁹~10⁻⁶¹；压缩真空态下退相干指数Γ_inf=(π/20)Ω_inf(md²H₀/ħ)²，最大约10⁻²⁷。

Conclusion: 辐射引力子引起的路径退相干在当前物质波干涉仪中完全可忽略，量子引力效应在现有实验精度下无法被探测。

Abstract: We derive which-path decoherence for matter-wave interferometry that arises from tracing out the gravitons of linearized quantum gravity. Because the gravitons are driven linearly by the matter source, the branch-dependent evolution can be solved exactly, and the reduced coherence is given by the characteristic function of the initial graviton state, evaluated at the difference of the branch-induced field displacements. In vacuum, the decoherence exponent equals half the mean number of gravitons radiated by the difference source. For a smooth Gaussian trajectory, it reduces to $Γ_{\rm vac}=(8/15)\,Gm^2d^4/(\hbar c^5τ^4)$, ranging from $10^{-89}$ to $10^{-61}$ across representative matter-wave platforms. For a general squeezed graviton vacuum, we obtain an exact expression in which squeezing either suppresses or enhances the vacuum response, depending on the squeezing phase. For the strongly squeezed state produced by inflation, this expression reduces to $Γ_{\rm inf}=(π/20)\,Ω_{\rm inf}(md^2H_0/\hbar)^2$ and reaches at most $\sim10^{-27}$ for optimistic parameters. Hence, the which-path decoherence induced by radiative gravitons is therefore negligible in current matter-wave interferometers.

</details>


### [118] [Using horizon shadows to distinguish a black hole and a white hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20945)
*Chengyu Bi,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 Kerr 度规框架下，通过广义相对论射线追踪和偏振计算，对比分析黑洞与白洞阶段的吸积盘成像差异，发现白洞图像呈现非对称嵌套强度环结构及环间偏振不连续性，为未来 VLBI 区分两类天体提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在圈量子引力等理论框架下，黑洞可能通过量子弹跳演化为白洞。为了在观测上区分黑洞与白洞，需要计算并对比两者在电磁波及偏振波段的成像特征，克服形态观测简并性。

Method: 采用广义相对论射线追踪技术，计算 Kerr 黑洞及反弹后 Kerr 白洞阶段吸积盘的成像；引入偏振射线追踪，分析偏振矢量在穿过白洞视界及内部时空时受引力场和自转拖曳影响后的偏振图像特征。

Result: 黑洞图像呈现新月形发射环和中心阴影；白洞图像因来自前一宇宙的辐射穿透旋转白洞，在框架拖曳和透镜效应作用下形成偏心且非对称的嵌套强度环结构。白洞偏振图像呈现空间旋转图案、同心干涉条纹及显著的环间偏振不连续性，与黑洞偏振行为不同。

Conclusion: 强度环结构和环间偏振不连续性为多波段及偏振干涉测量提供了克服形态观测简并性的基线，为未来 VLBI 区分黑洞与白洞提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Within theoretical frameworks such as loop quantum gravity, black holes may evolve into white holes through a quantum bounce. This paper uses general relativistic ray-tracing techniques to calculate the ray-traced imaging of accretion disks from the previous cosmic stage during the Kerr black hole and post-bounce Kerr white hole phases. Calculations show that the black hole image presents a crescent emission ring and a central shadow. In contrast, after radiation from the previous universe penetrates the rotating white hole, eccentric and asymmetric nested intensity ring structures form in the synthetic image due to frame-dragging and lensing effects. We analyze the influence of spin parameters, observation inclinations, and accretion disk geometric configurations on the distribution of this nested ring structure using synthetic images and intensity profiles. Building upon this, we introduce polarized ray-tracing calculations for radiation across evolutionary stages. This process results in the polarization image features after the polarization vector is subjected to the gravitational field and spacetime spin dragging during the photon propagation through the white hole horizon and internal spacetime. The spatial rotation patterns and concentric interference fringes in the white hole polarization images exhibit a distinct inter-ring polarization discontinuity. This phenomenon differs from the polarization behavior of black holes. The intensity ring structures and polarization inter-ring discontinuity features provide multi-band and polarimetric interferometry baselines to overcome morphological observational degeneracies. This provides theoretical guidance for future very-long-baseline interferometry (VLBI) to distinguish black holes and white holes.

</details>


### [119] [New generalization of the Barboza-Alcaniz parametrization of Dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21064)
*Shahab Shahidi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Barboza-Alcaniz暗能量参数化的推广形式，这是一个三参数模型，能解决原模型在未来时间的行为缺陷。宇宙学数据表明新参数化优于原模型和ΛCDM模型，其定性行为与原模型相似但加速率略小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原Barboza-Alcaniz暗能量参数化在未来时间存在行为缺陷，需要改进以更好地描述暗能量的演化特性。

Method: 提出一个三参数推广模型，通过引入额外参数修正原模型在未来时间的行为，并利用宇宙学观测数据进行拟合比较。

Result: 宇宙学数据支持新参数化优于原Barboza-Alcaniz模型和ΛCDM模型，新模型的定性行为与原模型相似但加速率略小。

Conclusion: 所提出的三参数推广模型能有效解决原模型的缺陷，且与观测数据符合更好，为暗能量研究提供了更优的参数化方案。

Abstract: A generalization of the Barboza-Alcaniz parametrization of dark energy is proposed. This is a three-parameter model which can resolve the shortcomings of the Barboza-Alcaniz behavior at future times. We show that cosmological data favor the new parametrization over both the Barboza-Alcaniz model and $Λ$CDM. We also consider the cosmological implications of the model and show that the qualitative behavior mimics to the original Barboza-Alcaniz model, with a slightly smaller acceleration rate.

</details>


### [120] [The leading-soft cubic graviton self-interaction on the black-hole horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21066)
*Ayanendu Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在GGV近地平线框架的偶Regge-Wheeler规范下，将爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量展开到Schwarzschild视界附近的立方阶，导出了立方引力子自相互作用。核心发现是“消失定理”：在领头软阶，纯无迹纵向极化自耦合恒为零。该定理是框架特定的，而非规范不变的一般结果。作者在IBM Qiskit/Aer上模拟了所得哈密顿量的实时动力学，验证了纵向通道的微扰刚性，并测量了残余修饰。所有电路结果与精确对角化一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量到Schwarzschild视界附近的立方阶，研究引力子自相互作用的性质，特别是纵向极化的软行为。

Method: 在GGV近地平线框架的偶Regge-Wheeler规范下，将爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量展开至立方阶，推导引力子自耦合；通过封闭形式约化证明消失定理；在IBM Qiskit/Aer上模拟实时动力学，并与精确对角化交叉验证。

Result: 发现“消失定理”：在领头软阶纯无迹纵向极化自耦合恒为零；在ℓ=2多重态上仅有~20%软/方案系统误差；模拟表明纵向通道微扰刚性，残余修饰deff=1.06，多重性远离热、泊松和Haar参考值；对称性精确的截断给出首个扇区分辨的能级统计。

Conclusion: 该定理是框架特定的，并非规范不变；纵向通道具有微扰刚性；所有结果均附带明确的系统误差，硬件执行被推迟到量化噪声预算之后。

Abstract: We expand the Einstein-Hilbert action to cubic order about the Schwarzschild horizon, in the even Regge-Wheeler gauge of the Gaddam-Groenenboom-'t~Hooft (GGV) near-horizon framework, and derive the cubic graviton self-interaction. Our central result is a `vanishing theorem': at leading soft order the self-coupling of the purely traceless longitudinal polarizations is identically zero, because with the trace/transverse-scalar sector switched off the fluctuation reduces to a two-dimensional block whose $\sqrt{-g}\,R$ is a total (Euler) derivative at every order in $κ$. We prove this by an explicit closed-form reduction and exhibit the cancellation term by term. It is a `framework-specific' statement, even RW gauge, GGV sector, leading soft order, not a gauge-invariant theorem of general relativity. The theorem is exact, but the quantized vertex inherits a $\sim\!20\%$ soft/scheme systematic at the only simulable multiplet ($\ell=2$), which we state explicitly. We then simulate the real-time dynamics of the resulting Hamiltonian on IBM Qiskit/Aer with exact cross-checks. Two structural facts, a conserved charge that only the cubic vertex violates, opening $φφ\to hh\to4φ$, and a provably resonance-free boost spectrum (gap $\to1/2$), already predict that the longitudinal channel is perturbatively rigid; the simulation confirms this quantitatively and measures the residual dressing ($d_{\rm eff}=1.06$; multiplicity far from thermal, Poisson, and Haar references) rather than discovering it. A symmetry-exact total-occupation truncation yields the first sector-resolved level statistics, indicative of intermediate behaviour on Hilbert spaces too small to be decisive. All circuit results agree with exact diagonalization, every headline number carries a stated systematic, and hardware execution is deferred behind a quantified noise budget.

</details>


### [121] [Spin-mediated modulation of chaos bound violation in Lorentz-violating black hole spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21165)
*Deyou Chen,Chuang Yang,Bingbing Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Bumblebee引力框架下，研究了粒子自旋如何调节Lorentz破缺黑洞时空中混沌边界的违反。结果表明自旋提供了一种精细调节机制，Lorentz破缺参数抑制违反，而宇宙学常数呈现双重影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粒子自旋在Lorentz破缺黑洞时空中对混沌边界违反的调节机制，揭示自旋-曲率耦合、Lorentz对称性破缺和宇宙学常数在混沌动力学中的作用。

Method: 在Bumblebee引力框架下，分析静态球对称(A)dS黑洞中带旋带电粒子的动力学行为，研究自旋-曲率耦合对混沌边界违反的影响。

Result: 自旋通过修改临界角动量阈值提供了混沌边界违反的精细调节机制；Lorentz破缺参数通过缩小允许参数区域和降低临界电荷来抑制违反；宇宙学常数具有双重影响：更负的宇宙学常数减小违反参数区域但增强违反幅度。

Conclusion: 自旋和Lorentz对称性破缺效应共同修改了轨道不稳定性与视界性质之间的关系，为理解引力系统的混沌动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate how the particle spin modulates chaos bound violations in Lorentz-violating black hole spacetimes within the framework of Bumblebee gravity. By analyzing spinning charged particles in static spherically symmetric (A)dS black holes, we reveal the roles of spin--curvature coupling, Lorentz symmetry breaking, and the cosmological constant in shaping chaotic dynamics. The results show that the spin provides a fine-tuning mechanism for the chaos bound violation by modifying the critical angular momentum threshold, allowing particles with larger spin to access the violation regime at lower angular momenta. The Lorentz-violating parameter suppresses the violations by narrowing the allowed parameter region and reducing the critical charge. Meanwhile, the cosmological constant exhibits a dual influence: a more negative cosmological constant decreases the parameter region of violation while enhancing the violation magnitude for configurations that remain beyond the bound. These findings demonstrate that the spin and Lorentz symmetry breaking effects jointly modify the relationship between orbital instability and horizon properties.

</details>


### [122] [Zipoy--Voorhees spacetime with Maxwell and dilaton fields: exact solution and equatorial geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21283)
*Haryanto M. Siahaan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过uplift-boost-reduction方法，从Zipoy-Voorhees真空种子构造了静态轴对称渐进平坦的Zipoy-Voorhees-dilaton时空，研究了其黑洞解释（k=1）、裸奇点（k≠1）、质量电荷和标量荷的代数约束，以及赤道面零测地线和光子的运动学性质，包括外光子环阈值（k=1/2）、ISCO轨道、引力红移和临界碰撞参数等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Zipoy-Voorhees真空解推广到带标量场（dilaton）的Kaluza-Klein理论中，构造新的精确解并研究其几何和测地线性质，特别是形变参数k和电荷参数α对时空结构的影响。

Method: 采用uplift-boost-reduction程序：先将四维真空种子解提升到五维，在五维中施加Lorentz boost变换，再约化回四维得到带dilaton场和电磁场的解。通过ADM分解提取质量、电荷和标量荷，利用哈密顿-雅可比方法分离变量求解测地线方程。

Result: 发现dilaton荷满足与四极形变无关的代数约束；外光子环阈值在k=1/2处对所有α成立；ISCO轨道在(k,α)平面上的分布依赖于是用种子质量还是ADM质量作为单位；引力红移在r=2M面发散；临界碰撞参数随电荷增大而减小。

Conclusion: 该解提供了研究形变带电致密天体的精确模型，揭示了dilaton场对四极形变、光子环和阴影尺寸的修正，且ADM质量单位下的阴影尺寸随电荷增长而小于Schwarzschild值，具有潜在的可观测检验意义。

Abstract: We derive a new exact solution of four-dimensional Kaluza-Klein theory by applying the uplift-boost-reduction procedure to the Zipoy-Voorhees vacuum seed. The resulting Zipoy-Voorhees-dilaton spacetime is a static, axisymmetric, asymptotically flat configuration parametrised by a seed length scale $M$, the Zipoy-Voorhees deformation parameter $k$, and a boost parameter $α$ that controls the electric charge. For $k=1$ it admits a regular black-hole interpretation; for $k \neq 1$ it inherits the naked curvature singularity of the seed at $r=2M$. We extract the ADM mass, electric charge, and dilaton charge, finding that the dilaton charge obeys an algebraic constraint independent of the quadrupole deformation. The equatorial geodesics of neutral test particles and photons are studied in detail. A compact algebraic identity locates the exterior-photon-ring threshold at $k=1/2$ for all $α$, generalising the known threshold of the vacuum Zipoy-Voorhees case. The innermost stable circular orbit is mapped over the $(k,α)$ plane, and the comparison with Schwarzschild is shown to depend qualitatively on whether seed-mass or ADM-mass units are used. We further compute the gravitational redshift, which diverges on the $r=2M$ surface and renders the near-singularity orbits effectively invisible, and the critical impact parameter setting the characteristic shadow size, which in ADM-mass units shrinks below the Schwarzschild value as the charge grows.

</details>


### [123] [Cosmological initial data without periodic boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21308)
*Károly Csukás*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文采用爱因斯坦约束条件的抛物-双曲形式生成宇宙学初始数据。与需要边界条件的标准椭圆方法不同，该方法从原点正则数据向外演化约束方程，无需边界条件，且能保证解的惟一性。数值实现了含有局部各向异性理想流体扰动的宇宙学初始数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准椭圆求解器在处理多解问题时可能失败，且常用的周期性等边界条件可能引入系统性偏差。作者希望提出一种无需边界条件、能保证解惟一的替代方法，以研究边界条件带来的偏差。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦约束条件的抛物-双曲（parabolic-hyperbolic）公式，从原点（r=0）的正则数据向外径向演化约束方程，而非求解椭圆型偏微分方程。自由规范几何数据取为平坦FLRW背景，物质部分包含局部各向异性的理想流体扰动。

Result: 数值实现了该方法，成功生成了包含局部各向异性理想流体扰动的宇宙学初始数据。

Conclusion: 该方法无需边界条件，解惟一，适合作为研究常见边界条件（如周期性边界）引入系统性偏差的起点。

Abstract: We apply the parabolic-hyperbolic formulation of the Einstein constraint equations to generate cosmological initial data. The freely specifiable geometric data correspond to flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker background, while the matter sector contains localized anisotropic perturbations of a perfect fluid. Unlike the standard approach, our method evolves the constraints outward from regular data at the origin and therefore requires no boundary conditions. This makes our method well suited as a starting point in investigating systematic biases introduced by commonly adopted boundary conditions, such as periodic boundaries. A second advantage concerns uniqueness: standard elliptic solvers may fail when multiple solutions exist, whereas solving the constraints as a well-posed evolutionary system always yields a unique solution. To demonstrate our method, we implement it numerically and generate cosmological initial data with localized anisotropic perfect fluid perturbations.

</details>


### [124] [Octupole moments and the non-universality of free-fall in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21314)
*Abraham I. Harte,Paul Ramond*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论中八极矩对自由落体非普适性的贡献，发现16个八极矩分量不参与运动方程，在牛顿时空和Petrov D类时空中八极矩具有与四极矩不同的定性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论中，延展体不一定沿测地线运动，其加速度取决于多极矩。不同物体即使初始数据相同，由于多极矩演化不同，下落方式也不同。本文旨在研究八极矩如何贡献于这种自由落体的非普适性。

Method: 首先证明在任意真空时空中，只有无迹部分的八极矩影响物体运动，由此40个分量中至少16个解耦。然后分别基于类时标架矢量和零标架获得无迹八极矩的两种分解，并将其应用于牛顿时空和Petrov D类真空时空中的运动分析。

Result: 在牛顿时空中，质量矩呈现普通牛顿效应，动量矩决定物体的隐藏动量（动量与速度的错位）。在Petrov D类时空中（如Kerr），八极矩可实现四极矩无法实现的扭矩，产生定性不同的效应。

Conclusion: 八极矩在广义相对论自由落体非普适性中扮演重要角色，其效应与低阶多极矩有本质区别，尤其在强引力场中可能产生新的物理现象。

Abstract: Extended bodies in general relativity do not necessarily fall along geodesics, but can be accelerated. These accelerations depend on an object's angular momentum, as well as on its quadrupole, octupole, and higher-order moments. However, these multipole moments can evolve differently from one body to another. Different bodies can thus fall differently, even with identical initial data. This paper examines how octupole moments contribute to the non-universality of free-fall in general relativity. We begin by showing that in arbitrary vacuum spacetimes, only the trace-free component of an octupole moment can affect an object's motion. It follows that at least 16 out of 40 octupole components decouple from the laws of motion. Then, we obtain two decompositions for trace-free octupole moments, one in terms of a timelike frame vector and the other in terms of a null tetrad. These decompositions are applied to motion both in generic Newtonian spacetimes and in fully-relativistic vacuum spacetimes that are of Petrov type D. In Newtonian spacetimes, the mass moments are shown to have their ordinary Newtonian effects, while the momentum moments determine a body's hidden momentum---the misalignment between its momentum and its velocity. In Petrov type D spacetimes (such as Kerr), we show that some torques that are impossible with quadrupole moments are possible with octupole moments. Octupole moments can thus have qualitatively-different effects from quadrupole moments.

</details>


### [125] [A story about a tipsy kangaroo: Reversible jump MCMC for model selection in the analysis of gravitational-wave signals from the coalescence of compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21484)
*Anna Puecher,Tim Dietrich,Hauke Koehn,Jonathan Gair,Gregory Ashton,Luca Negri,Anuradha Samajdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了t-roo，一种用于引力波信号分析的 reversible jump MCMC 采样器，能够同时进行模型比较和参数估计，具有显著的计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波信号分析中，模型选择对理解源物理和指导模型发展至关重要。传统方法需分别用不同模型分析数据并比较贝叶斯证据，计算成本高，尤其在模型数量多或数据信息量大的情况下。因此需要一种更高效的跨维度推断方法。

Method: 基于 eryn 采样器开发了 t-roo，一种 reversible jump Markov chain Monte Carlo (RJMCMC) 采样器，可以直接在模型空间上进行采样，同时获得模型胜率比和参数后验分布。能够比较不同类型源（BBH、BNS、NSBH）以及同一源类下的多种模型。

Result: 在模拟注入数据上验证了采样器的有效性，结果与嵌套采样器 dynesty 一致。随后用于分析真实事件 GW190425 和 GW230529，这些事件仅凭系统参数无法提供中子星成分存在的确凿证据。

Conclusion: t-roo 可适用于任何模型比较场景，尤其对下一代引力波探测器具有重要意义，因为传统分别分析方法计算成本将更加高昂。

Abstract: Bayesian inference is commonly employed in the analysis of gravitational-wave signals not only to estimate the source parameters, but also for model selection. The latter provides insight into the physics of the source and has the potential to inform the direction for future model development. Although model comparison is usually performed by analyzing the data separately with different models and comparing the obtained Bayesian evidences, an alternative approach consists in sampling directly over the model itself. Here, we present t-roo, a reversible jump Markov chain Monte Carlo sampler capable of performing transdimensional inference on gravitational-wave signals from compact binary coalescences. Employing t-roo, a single analysis provides simultaneously the model odds ratio and the parameter posteriors for the favored models, hence yielding a potentially substantial computational advantage, particularly when comparing many models or analyzing highly informative data. t-roo is built on the sampler eryn and is specifically designed to compare models describing different kinds of sources, i.e., binary black hole, binary neutron star, or neutron star-black hole systems, as well as multiple models for the same source class. We validate the sampler on a set of injections, finding agreement with the results obtained with the nested sampler dynesty. We then use t-roo to analyze the real events GW190425 and GW230529, for which the system's parameters alone do not provide conclusive evidence of the presence of a neutron star component. t-roo can be adapted to any model-comparison scenario, thus providing a valuable tool in particular for next-generation detectors, where analyzing data separately with competing models becomes computationally even more demanding.

</details>


### [126] [Breaking the Degeneracy: Spectral Hardening of Accretion Disks around Rotating Hayward Black Holes in Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14164)
*Sandip Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转规则Hayward黑洞周围薄吸积盘在暗物质晕中的热力学和观测特征，发现量子修正的规则核心与暗物质晕对辐射效率具有叠加增强效应，但在低频段存在几何与天体物理构型的简并性，唯有通过极端Wien尾的高频光谱才能打破这种简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究旋转规则Hayward黑洞嵌入暗物质晕后，吸积盘的ISCO、辐射效率、热流和光谱特征如何变化，并揭示量子修正黑洞与经典暗物质环境之间的观测简并性及其破解途径。

Method: 将时空严格建模为三区域复合结构：内部Hayward核心（de Sitter极限正则化）、中间暗物质壳层（指数球密度分布的无压尘埃包层）以及外部渐近平坦真空。通过质量轮廓匹配各区域，计算ISCO、辐射效率、局域热通量及多色黑体光谱辐射率，并在四种构型（Kerr真空、Hayward真空、Kerr+DM、Hayward+DM）间进行系统比较。

Result: 量子规则核心与暗物质晕对ISCO产生严格层次化压缩；对高自旋黑洞（j=0.8），辐射效率可达~14.4%；低-中频段存在纯几何（Hayward真空）与纯天体物理（Kerr+DM）构型之间的宏观简并性。

Conclusion: 该简并性仅在多波长光谱的极端Wien尾中被结构性地打破，为未来高频旋谱偏振测量区分非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境提供了关键诊断指标。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic and observable signatures of thin accretion disks surrounding rotating, regular Hayward black holes embedded within a macroscopic dark matter (DM) envelope. The spacetime is rigorously modelled as a three-region composite: an inner Hayward core regularised by a de~Sitter limit, an intermediate DM shell modelled as a pressureless dust envelope governed by an exponential sphere density profile, and an asymptotically flat outer vacuum. By matching these regions via the mass profile, we compute the explicit modifications to the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO), radiative efficiency, local thermal flux, and multi-colour blackbody spectral luminosity. A systematic comparison across four distinct configurations (Kerr vacuum, Hayward vacuum, Kerr\,+\,DM, and Hayward\,+\,DM) reveals a strict hierarchical compression of the ISCO. We demonstrate that the quantum-inspired core regularity and the macroscopic DM halo exert an additive enhancement on the radiative efficiency, reaching $\sim 14.4\%$ for highly spinning black holes ($j=0.8$). However, this additive behaviour introduces a profound macroscopic degeneracy between purely geometric (Hayward vacuum) and purely astrophysical (Kerr\,+\,DM) configurations at low-to-intermediate frequencies. We establish that this degeneracy is structurally broken only in the extreme Wien tail of the multi-wavelength spectrum, providing a critical diagnostic footprint for future high-frequency spectropolarimetry to distinguish non-singular black holes from classical dark matter environments.

</details>

### [127] [An FBSDE Construction of the Sine-Gordon EQFT for $β^{2} < \frac{6}{7}\, 8π$ and Perturbative Renormalization in the Full Subcritical Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20632)
*Sarah-Jean Meyer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限体积下构造了sine-Gordon测度及其重整化有效势，达到第七阈值。该构造依赖于弱随机控制问题、相关弱FBSDE，以及使用多极Mayer展开分析重整化流动方程。分析是完全归纳的，并在次临界区域β²<8π中包含了完整的逐阶分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造sine-Gordon测度及其重整化有效势，特别是在有限体积下达到第七阈值，以深入理解该量子场论模型的数学结构。

Method: 采用弱随机控制问题及其相关的弱前向后向随机微分方程（FBSDE），并结合多极Mayer展开分析重整化流动方程。整个分析过程是完全归纳的，在次临界区域β²<8π中进行逐阶分析。

Result: 成功在有限体积下构造了sine-Gordon测度及其重整化有效势，达到了第七阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作为sine-Gordon模型在有限体积下的数学构造提供了更深入的进展，验证了所提方法在次临界区域的有效性。

Abstract: We construct the sine-Gordon measure and its renormalized effective potential in finite volume up to the seventh threshold. The construction relies on a weak stochastic control problem, its associated weak FBSDE, and an analysis of the renormalization flow equation using a multipole Mayer expansion. The analysis is fully inductive, and we include a complete order-by-order analysis in the subcritical regime $β^{2}<8π$.

</details>


### [128] [Affine Extension of the Free-Particle--Oscillator--Inverted-Oscillator Triangle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20711)
*Andrey Alcala,Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了一维线性势系统作为自由粒子、谐振子(HO)和反谐振子(IHO)构成的共形三角的仿射扩展。线性势哈密顿量涉及薛定谔代数的海森堡理想，与自由粒子通过正则加速系变换关联，而HO和IHO实现则通过奇异位移振子极限得到。Airy能量本征态来自自由粒子动量本征态的立方相变换、高激发谐振子能级的凝聚以及反谐振子亚主导抛物柱面散射分支的极限。在均匀交叉电磁场中的平面扩展中，均匀加速度产生电相互作用，均匀旋转产生朗道耦合和离心反谐振子项，引导中心动力学产生霍尔漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性势系统作为共形三角形的仿射扩展，揭示其与自由粒子、谐振子和反谐振子之间的深层联系，以及它们在经典和量子层面的统一描述。

Method: 采用加速系变换、经典作用量规范变换、波函数纠缠变换和传播子方法，结合奇异位移振子极限和立方相变换等技术手段。

Result: 建立了线性势系统与自由粒子、HO、IHO之间的严格变换关系，给出了Airy本征态的三种不同构造途径，并在电磁场平面扩展中实现了对霍尔漂移等物理效应的统一描述。

Conclusion: 线性势系统作为仿射扩展丰富了共形量子力学的结构，为理解不同量子系统之间的变换关系提供了统一框架，并可用于描述均匀电磁场中的带电粒子动力学。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional linear-potential system as an affine extension of the conformal triangle formed by the free particle, harmonic oscillator (HO) and inverted harmonic oscillator (IHO). Unlike these homogeneous quadratic Hamiltonians in the $sl(2,\mathbb R)$ sector, the linear-potential Hamiltonian involves the Heisenberg ideal of the Schrödinger algebra. Its direct relation to the free particle is a regular accelerated-frame transformation, developed here at the levels of the classical action, canonical transformation, wave-function intertwiner and propagator; its HO and IHO realizations instead arise through singular displaced-oscillator limits. The Airy energy eigenstates follow from a cubic-phase transform of free-particle momentum eigenstates, from condensation of highly excited harmonic-oscillator levels, and from the limit of a subdominant parabolic-cylinder scattering branch of the inverted oscillator. We also develop a planar extension in homogeneous crossed electric and magnetic fields, where uniform acceleration generates the electric interaction, while uniform rotation produces the Landau coupling and the centrifugal inverted-oscillator term; the guiding-center dynamics yields the Hall drift.

</details>


### [129] [High-resolution decomposition of canonical Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20740)
*Kaja Krhac,Frederic P. Schuller*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别了哈密顿系统具有端口-哈密顿重构的拓扑和动力学条件，此类重构能揭示哈密顿理论中隐藏的结构细节。研究发现，可达到的分辨率随正则哈密顿系统配置空间分解的因子流形数量呈指数增长，且仅受原始哈密顿量的简并性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈密顿理论无法揭示系统内部的一些结构细节，因此作者希望从拓扑和动力学角度探索哈密顿系统是否能够通过端口-哈密顿重构来获得更高分辨率的结构描述。

Method: 通过识别哈密顿系统具备端口-哈密顿重构的拓扑与动力学条件，分析配置空间分解为因子流形的数量与可达到的分辨率之间的关系，并考虑原始哈密顿量简并性的影响。

Result: 可达到的结构分辨率随因子流形数量呈指数增长，但受到原始哈密顿量简并性的制约。

Conclusion: 端口-哈密顿重构能够揭示哈密顿理论中隐藏的结构细节，且其分辨率在理论上可以随系统分解的复杂度指数级提升，从而为更精细的系统建模与分析提供了可能。

Abstract: We identify the topological and dynamical conditions for a Hamiltonian system to possess port-Hamiltonian reformulations with increasing resolution of structural detail that remains hidden in the Hamiltonian theory. We find that the principally attainable resolution grows exponentially with the number of factor manifolds into which the configuration space of a canonical Hamiltonian system decomposes and is tempered only by degeneracies of the original Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [130] [An Operator-Algebraic Exposition of the Thirring-Wehrl Theory of the Quasi-Spin BCS Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21158)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文用C*-代数语言重新表述并统一了BCS理论的多种数学分析，建立了从微观模型到热力学极限的完备框架，并揭示了规范对称性破缺与中心分解的收敛机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一和严格化BCS理论中Haag、Emch-Guenin、Thirring-Wehrl等人的分析以及后续Bóna、Raggio-Werner、Bru-de Siqueira Pedra等人的算子代数平均场结果，从而在更一般的数学语言下处理强极限、域限制极限和扇区wise Bogoliubov-Haag极限等概念。

Method: 采用准局域C*-代数与UHF型2^∞代数的数学框架，利用von Neumann不完全无限张量积刻画乘积扇区，通过中心直接积分分解处理规范平均，并将规范相位作为中心经典变量引入，将相位依赖的Bogoliubov动力学统一为C(S^1, A)上的自同构群。

Result: 成功区分了强度量的强极限、广延量的域限制极限以及扇区wise的Bogoliubov-Haag极限，给出了相应的算子判据、能隙方程和极限动力学；在简并模型中，基态和热规范平均可在规范圆上实现中心直接积分分解，且热分解在零温下收敛到基态分解。

Conclusion: 该综述通过准局域C*-代数与态分解的语言，统一了BCS理论的不同数学形式，建立了从微观量子模型到宏观热力学极限的严格框架，并揭示了规范对称性破缺与中心分解在零温极限下的收敛机制。

Abstract: This expository review reformulates the BCS analyses of Haag, Emch--Guenin, and Thirring--Wehrl, together with subsequent operator-algebraic mean-field results of Bóna, Raggio--Werner, and Bru--de Siqueira Pedra, in the language of quasi-local $C^{\ast}$-algebras and state decompositions. The quasi-spin model is realized on the UHF algebra of type $2^{\infty}$, with product sectors described by von Neumann's incomplete infinite tensor products. This framework distinguishes strong limits of intensive observables, domain-restricted limits of extensive observables, and sectorwise Bogoliubov--Haag limits, including their operator criterion, gap equation, and limiting dynamics. In the degenerate model, the ground-state and thermal gauge averages admit central direct-integral decompositions over the gauge circle, and the thermal decomposition converges to the ground-state decomposition at zero temperature. Adjoining the gauge phase as a central classical variable combines the phase-dependent Bogoliubov dynamics into one automorphism group on $C(\mathbb{S}^{1},\mathcal{A})$.

</details>


### [131] [Continuum limit of discretized matrix-valued Fourier multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21177)
*Ruben Karapetyan,Matěj Tušek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Cornean, Garde和Jensen关于离散Dirac算子连续极限的结果，研究了一类广泛的块算子矩阵（包括双层石墨烯哈密顿量），分析了连续算子与其特定离散版本在网格参数趋零时的广义范数预解收敛性，发现部分离散化方案需要添加修正项才能保证收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Cornean等人关于离散Dirac算子连续极限的最新成果，将其推广到更一般的块算子矩阵类别，以理解更广泛的物理模型（如双层石墨烯）中连续与离散描述之间的关系。

Method: 通过建立连续算子与特定嵌入到连续空间中的离散算子的预解式之间的范数估计，分析不同离散化方案下的收敛行为，并识别需要添加修正项才能保证收敛的情况。

Result: 部分离散化方案能够直接实现广义范数预解收敛，而其他方案则需要引入合适的修正项才能确保收敛。

Conclusion: 不同离散化方案在趋近连续极限时表现不同，有些需要额外的修正处理，这为理解块算子矩阵（特别是双层石墨烯等物理系统）的离散-连续对应关系提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Building upon a recent result by H. Cornean, H. Garde, and A. Jensen concerning continuum limits of discrete Dirac operators, we extend the analysis to a wide class of block operator matrices. This class includes, among others, the bilayer graphene Hamiltonian. Our main goal is to find norm estimates for the difference between the resolvents of continuous operators and their discrete counterparts embedded in the continuum in a specific way. While some discretization schemes lead directly to convergence in the generalized norm resolvent sense as the mesh parameter tends to zero, others require the addition of a suitable correction term to ensure the convergence.

</details>


### [132] [Representation of the causal logic for the Dirac system and the electron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21266)
*Domenico P. L. Castrigiano,Carmine De Rosa,Valter Moretti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于狄拉克概率流和一般性的流到局域化方法，为狄拉克系统和电子构建了因果逻辑的协变表示，证明了其在欧几里得空间与牛顿-温格算符的关系，并揭示了位置测量诱导正电子产生的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为狄拉克系统和电子构建协变的因果逻辑表示，解决相对论性量子系统中局域化与因果性的兼容问题。

Method: 基于守恒的狄拉克概率流和一般性的流到局域化方法（适用于非时区域），通过非平稳相位论证推导多项式衰减估计，验证全狄拉克系统协变非时局域化的条件，并将其扩展至完整时空区域以构建因果逻辑表示。

Result: 欧几里得空间中狄拉克局域化与典型投影值局域化一致，位置算符与牛顿-温格算符相差一个有界自伴修正；全狄拉克非时局域化是投影值的且满足微观因果性；压缩至电子子空间后变为正算子值但保持因果性；高能极限下获得洛伦兹收缩的概率解释；位置测量诱导正电子产生是普适的。

Conclusion: 成功构建了狄拉克系统与电子的协变因果逻辑表示，建立了局域化、因果性与测量诱导粒子产生之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We construct a covariant representation of the causal logic for the Dirac system and the electron, based on the conserved Dirac probability current and a general current-to-localization procedure for achronal regions. A polynomial decay estimate, derived by a non-stationary phase argument, verifies the conditions required to define covariant achronal localization for the full Dirac system. Extending this localization to complete spacetime regions yields the corresponding causal-logic representation; restriction to the positive-energy invariant subspace gives the analogous construction for the electron. We establish several structural properties. On Euclidean space, Dirac localization agrees with canonical projection-valued localization, while its position operator differs from the Newton--Wigner operator by an explicitly determined bounded self-adjoint correction. Full Dirac achronal localization is projection valued and represents achronal separateness by orthogonality, thus satisfying microscopic causality. After compression to the electron subspace, it becomes positive-operator valued while preserving causality. We further extend known results from spatial to general achronal regions, prove separation and norm-one criteria, and analyze the high-boost limit, obtaining Lorentz contraction in a precise probabilistic sense. Finally, we discuss the electron--positron decomposition and the state transformation induced by position measurements, showing that measurement-induced positron production is universal and independent of the specific measuring device.

</details>


### [133] [On Leray's Theoreme de Structure for a weak solution to the Navier-Stokes equations: an improvement of the result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21380)
*Paolo Maremonti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Navier-Stokes方程弱解的Leray结构定理，给出了关于正则性无界区间左端点的新估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Leray经典结构定理中关于弱解正则性区间的估计，特别是无界正则区间左端点的上界。

Method: 基于Leray结构定理的分析框架，通过新的估计技术对无界正则区间的左端点进行更精确的界定。

Result: 得到了关于无界正则区间左端点的新估计量。

Conclusion: 该结果改进了对Navier-Stokes方程弱解正则性结构的理解，为后续研究提供了更精确的估计工具。

Abstract: In this note we furnish a new result concerning the well known théorème de structure by Leray related to a weak solution to the Navier-Stokes equations. We are able to furnish a new estimate on the left endpoint of the unbounded interval of regularity.

</details>


### [134] [Two-phase source and reaction coefficient Stefan type problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21388)
*Targyn A. Nauryz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有未知时变源项和反应系数的两相Stefan型反问题，通过变换和傅里叶谱展开推导重建公式，并证明了弱解和强解的存在性、唯一性、有界性和正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两相相变过程中未知时变热参数（源系数和反应系数）的识别问题，为实际相变过程提供理论依据。

Method: 采用变换将自由边界问题转化为固定域上的等价方程，利用傅里叶谱展开推导未知系数的重建公式；第一类问题通过积分、逐点和非局部附加数据识别源系数，导出Volterra积分方程；第二类问题应用指数变换恢复反应系数；利用两个移动边界各自的Stefan条件确定两个未知系数而无需额外超定条件。

Result: 建立了弱解和强解的存在性、唯一性、有界性和正则性；数值算例验证了重建过程的适用性，且系数在有噪声数据下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该方法为两相相变过程中时变热参数的识别提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates inverse two-phase Stefan-type problems for parabolic heat equations with unknown time-dependent source and reaction coefficients. Suitable transformations reduce the original free-boundary problems to equivalent equations on fixed spatial domains, and Fourier spectral expansions are used to derive reconstruction formulas for the unknown coefficients. In the first formulation, the source coefficients are identified from integral, pointwise, and nonlocal additional data, leading to Volterra integral equations. In the second formulation, an exponential transformation is applied to recover the reaction coefficients. A principal advantage of the model with two moving boundaries is that each boundary provides its own Stefan condition which sufficient to determine the two unknown time-dependent coefficients without additional overdetermination conditions. Under appropriate assumptions the existence, uniqueness, boundedness and regularity of weak and strong solutions are established. Illustrative examples confirm the applicability of the reconstruction procedure and show that the coefficients remain stable under the noisy data. The proposed approach provides a rigorous base for identifying time-dependent thermal parameters in two-phase phase-change processes.

</details>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [The Penrose conjecture for initial data sets satisfying a $2$-convexity condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20741)
*Conghan Dong*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特定条件下（渐近平坦初始数据集、主能量条件、2-凸性条件），彭罗斯猜想对边界的每个连通分量成立，主要工具是σ-逆平均曲率流及其单调性公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明广义相对论中彭罗斯猜想的特定情形，即在外表视界存在且满足主能量条件和2-凸性条件下，证明黑洞质量与视界面积极限的关系。

Method: 利用σ-逆平均曲率流（σ-inverse mean curvature flow）以及该流在文献[Dong26SigmaIMCF]中建立的单调性公式作为主要分析工具。

Result: 对每个边界连通分量，在满足主能量条件和2-凸性条件（k的两个最小特征值之和非负）的渐近平坦初始数据集上，证明了彭罗斯猜想。

Conclusion: 在特定几何条件下，彭罗斯猜想成立，推广了经典逆平均曲率流在彭罗斯不等式证明中的应用。

Abstract: Let $(M^3, g, \mathbf{k})$ be a smooth, connected, asymptotically flat initial data set with outermost past apparent horizon. We prove the Penrose conjecture for each connected component of the boundary, assuming the dominant energy condition and the $2$-convexity condition that the sum of the two smallest eigenvalues of $\mathbf{k}$ is nonnegative. The main tool is the $σ$-inverse mean curvature flow and a monotonicity formula developed in \cite{Dong26SigmaIMCF}.

</details>


### [136] [CmcMesh: a python implementation of the DPW method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20986)
*Thomas Raujouan,Martin Traizet*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CmcMesh，一个用Python实现的用于构造三维空间形式中极小曲面和常平均曲率曲面的开源软件包，基于Dorfmeister-Pedit-Wu方法，覆盖从全纯势到渲染曲面的完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为DPW方法提供一个模块化、可扩展的Python实现，简化从全纯势到渲染曲面的完整工作流程，并支持多种空间形式（R^3, H^3, S^3）中的极小曲面和CMC曲面构造。

Method: 基于Hoffman和Hoffman的Mesh算法，实现了Weierstrass表示（R^3中极小曲面）、Bryant表示（H^3中CMC-1曲面）以及DPW方法（R^3和S^3中CMC曲面及极小曲面）。采用模块化设计，便于扩展新的DPW变体。

Result: 成功开发了一个覆盖完整管线的Python软件包CmcMesh，能够从全纯势出发构造并渲染三维空间形式中的极小曲面和CMC曲面。

Conclusion: CmcMesh为DPW方法的数值实现提供了一个模块化、易扩展的Python工具，支持多种空间形式中的曲面构造，且便于后续添加新的DPW变体。

Abstract: Minimal and constant mean curvature (CMC) surfaces in three-dimensional space forms can be constructed with the Dorfmeister--Pedit--Wu (DPW) method. We introduce CmcMesh, a Python implementation covering the whole pipeline from holomorphic potential to rendered surfaces, using Hoffman and Hoffman's Mesh algorithm. We implement the Weierstrass representation for minimal surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$, Bryant's representation for CMC-1 surfaces in $\mathbb{H}^3$, and the DPW method for CMC surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and for minimal and CMC surfaces in $\mathbb{S}^3$. The package is modular, making it easy to add new DPW variants without modifying existing code.

</details>


### [137] [Structure theory of $BV$ functions and finite perimeter sets on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21031)
*Péter Koltai,Kathrin Völkner*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在无全局曲率界或完备性假设的任意黎曼流形上，建立了有界变差函数与有限周长集的结构理论，通过局部化框架融合欧氏几何测度论与度量测度空间分析，恢复了经典BV理论的关键结果，并应用于边值问题与毛细现象能量泛函的Gamma收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典欧氏空间中的有界变差函数与有限周长集的结构理论推广到任意黎曼流形上，摆脱对全局曲率界或流形完备性的依赖，为流形上的边值问题与变分问题提供理论框架。

Method: 构建局部化框架，融合欧氏几何测度论技术与度量测度空间分析方法，同时保留黎曼流形的特有结构（如极向量场、法向量场、简化边界、近似切空间）。

Result: 恢复了向量值测度的微分定理、De Giorgi与Federer结构定理等经典欧氏BV理论的关键结果；建立了流形上区域的迹定理与Gauss-Green定理；证明了有限周长集在严格意义下的逼近结果（满足给定Dirichlet边界条件），并将其应用于混合边界条件毛细现象能量泛函族的Gamma收敛性证明。

Conclusion: 成功将经典欧氏BV理论的大部分内容推广到黎曼流形上，为流形上的边界值问题与变分问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop the theory of functions of bounded variation and the structure theory of finite-perimeter sets on arbitrary Riemannian manifolds without relying on global curvature bounds or completeness of the manifold. To this end, we build a localization framework that permits a synthesis of techniques from Euclidean geometric measure theory and analysis on metric measure spaces while preserving genuinely Riemannian features, such as polar and normal vector fields, reduced boundaries, and approximate tangent spaces. As a consequence, we recover key results of the Euclidean theory, such as a differentiation theorem for a Riemannian generalization of vector-valued measures and the structure theorems by De Giorgi and Federer, formulated intrinsically on Riemannian manifolds. This makes a large portion of the classical Euclidean $BV$ theory available to the Riemannian setting.
  We demonstrate this by providing the theoretical background for boundary value problems on domains in manifolds, including trace and Gauss-Green theorems. Finally, we prove an approximation result for finite-perimeter sets in a strict sense that respects a prescribed Dirichlet boundary portion of a given ambient domain and apply this to prove Gamma-convergence of a family of energy functionals for capillarity problems with mixed boundary conditions.

</details>


### [138] [Weak elastic energy of rectifiable curves in Riemannian surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21056)
*Domenico Mucci,Alberto Saracco,Cristian Sopio*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文针对无边界紧致可定向光滑黎曼曲面上的可求长曲线，引入了一种弱弹性能量。该能量通过内接测地多边形p-旋转的松弛定义，利用归一化等温坐标的局部构造实现。对于每个指数p>1，松弛后的泛函能够精确检测曲线弧长参数化的内禀二阶Sobolev正则性。当松弛能量有限时，它等于测地曲率p次幂的积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为曲面上的可求长曲线定义一种能够检测内禀二阶Sobolev正则性的弱弹性能量，扩展经典弹性能量到更低正则性的曲线。

Method: 通过松弛方法，从内接测地多边形p-旋转的概念出发，在归一化等温坐标中进行局部构造来定义能量。

Result: 松弛后的泛函对于每个指数p>1能精确检测曲线弧长参数化的二阶Sobolev正则性，且当能量有限时等于测地曲率p次幂的积分。

Conclusion: 成功建立了一种适用于可求长曲线的弱弹性能量框架，将经典的测地曲率能量推广到更广泛的曲线类别，且保持了与二阶正则性的精确对应关系。

Abstract: We introduce a weak elastic energy for rectifiable curves on compact orientable smooth Riemannian surfaces without boundary. The energy is defined by relaxation starting from a notion of $p$-rotation of inscribed geodesic polygonals, that is obtained by a local construction in normalized isothermal coordinates. For every exponent $p>1$, the resulting relaxed functional detects precisely the intrinsic second-order Sobolev regularity of the arc-length parameterization of the curve. Furthermore, when the relaxed energy is finite, it agrees with the integral of the $p$-power of the geodesic curvature.

</details>


### [139] [Lu's conjecture for minimal surfaces in codimension two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21336)
*Jianquan Ge,Fagui Li,Yunheng Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了若二维极小浸入曲面$M^2\to\Sph^4$满足$S+\lambda_2$为大于2的常数，则$S+\lambda_2\geq3$，从而证实了Lu的第二间隙猜想在余维数为2时的情形。结合超曲面情形及高余维反例，完成了该猜想在所有余维数下的完整刻画：当余维数$m=1,2$时成立，当$m\geq3$时均不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Lu提出的第二间隙猜想在余维数为2时的遗留情形，进而完整刻画该猜想在不同余维数下的成立与否，填补极小曲面理论中关于第二基本形式模长与Lu基本矩阵特征值之和的间隙现象的知识空白。

Method: 对闭极小浸入$M^2\to\Sph^4$，利用Lu基本矩阵的特征值$\lambda_1\geq\lambda_2\geq0$，分析$S+\lambda_2$为常数且大于2的条件，通过几何分析和不等式推导证明该常数下界为3。

Result: 在余维数2的情形下，若$S+\lambda_2$为大于2的常数，则必有$S+\lambda_2\geq3$。结合Peng-Terng的超曲面结果（余维1）和Li-Zhao的高余维反例（$m\geq3$），Lu的第二间隙猜想在$m=1,2$时成立，在$m\geq3$时均不成立。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Lu的第二间隙猜想在所有余维数下的成立性问题，即该猜想在余维数1和2时成立，在余维数≥3时均存在反例，从而完成了该领域的一个完整分类结果。

Abstract: Let $M^2\to\Sph^4$ be a closed minimal immersion, let $S$ be the squared norm of its second fundamental form, and let $λ_1\geqλ_2\geq0$ be the eigenvalues of Lu's fundamental matrix. We prove that if $S+λ_2$ is constant and larger than $2$, then $S+λ_2\geq3$. Thus Lu's second-gap conjecture holds for minimal surfaces in codimension two.
  Together with the hypersurface result of Peng--Terng and the counterexamples of Li--Zhao in every codimension $m\geq3$, our theorem completes the codimension picture of Lu's second-gap conjecture for minimal surfaces: it holds precisely for $m=1,2$ and fails for every $m\geq3$.

</details>


### [140] [Doubling for chronological diamonds in Lorentzian geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21423)
*Mauricio Che,Sebastian Gieger,Clemens Sämann*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在测度洛伦兹长度空间中定义了基于时间钟形区域的加倍条件，证明了该条件可由适当的类时曲率界限推导得出，首次将洛伦兹几何中的加倍性质与曲率界限联系起来，并由此获得了关于洛伦兹广义锥的紧致性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次在洛伦兹几何中建立加倍条件与类时曲率界限之间的关联，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Method: 在测度洛伦兹长度空间中，利用时间钟形区域（chronological diamonds）定义加倍条件，并通过类时曲率界限推导该条件成立。

Result: 证明了类时曲率界限蕴含所定义的加倍条件，并基于此获得了关于洛伦兹广义锥在Mondino-Sämann提出的洛伦兹Gromov-Hausdorff收敛意义下的紧致性结果。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了洛伦兹几何中加倍性质与曲率界限的联系，并为洛伦兹广义锥的紧致性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We define doubling conditions for measured Lorentzian length spaces in terms of chronological diamonds, and prove that such conditions are implied by suitable timelike curvature bounds. In particular, for the first time, we relate doubling to curvature bounds in Lorentzian geometry. As a consequence, we obtain compactness results for Lorentzian generalized cones with respect to the Lorentzian Gromov--Hausdorff convergence introduced by Mondino--Sämann.

</details>


### [141] [Isoparametric foliations of $S^n\times S^n$ of codimension $1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21500)
*Teng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了$S^n\times S^n$上等参叶状结构的新例子，证明了余维1的等参叶状结构可由双齐次多项式（双次数为(1,1),(2,2),(3,3),(4,4)或(6,6)）限制得到，并分类了双次数(1,1)和(2,2)的双齐次等参多项式以及$n$为偶数且$n\neq4$时的等参叶状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丰富$S^n\times S^n$上等参叶状结构的例子，并建立其基本性质与分类理论。

Method: 构造新例子；利用双齐次多项式刻画余维1等参叶状结构；通过代数分类方法对特定双次数的情形进行分类。

Result: 给出了若干族新的等参叶状结构例子；证明了余维1等参叶状结构可由双次数为(1,1),(2,2),(3,3),(4,4)或(6,6)的双齐次多项式的限制实现；完成了双次数(1,1)和(2,2)及$n$为偶数且$n\neq4$时的分类。

Conclusion: 在$S^n\times S^n$上建立了等参叶状结构与双齐次多项式之间的深刻联系，并取得了部分分类结果。

Abstract: In this article, we give several families of new examples of isoparametric foliations of $S^n\times S^n$; establish some basic properties of isoparametric foliations of $S^n\times S^n$ of codimension $1$, especially any of them can be realized as level sets of the restriction of a bi-homogeneous polynomial of bi-degree $(1,1),(2,2),(3,3),(4,4)$ or $(6,6)$ to $S^n\times S^n$; and classify bi-homogeneous isoparametric polynomials of bi-degree $(1,1)$ and $(2,2)$ and isoparametric foliations on $S^n\times S^n$ with $n$ even and not equal to $4$.

</details>


### [142] [Conformal Kähler rigidity of Einstein four-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21538)
*Giovanni Catino,Davide Dameno*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了紧致连通定向爱因斯坦四流形上，如果自对偶Weyl曲率$W^+$的最大特征值处处单重，则至多经过二重覆盖后，度量共形于具有正数量曲率的Kähler度量；该结果推广到具有调和自对偶Weyl曲率的度量。对于满足最大特征值一致单重假设的爱因斯坦度量，进一步证明要么$W^+\equiv 0$，要么$W^+$处处非零且前述结论成立。还扩展到完备Ricci平坦四流形，并得到紧致Kähler-Einstein曲面上全纯截面曲率的最优夹挤定理。证明结合了LeBrun共形归一化和加权散度方程及新的一阶恒等式。零容量方法使得该技术可穿越$W^+$的零点集及非紧情形下的无穷远点。K3曲面和多中心Gibbons-Hawking引力瞬子表明假设是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究爱因斯坦四流形上Weyl曲率特征值的几何约束，探索自对偶Weyl曲率最大特征值的单重性如何强制流形具有共形Kähler结构，并建立最优的曲率夹挤定理。

Method: 结合LeBrun的共形归一化技巧与加权散度方程，推导新的一阶恒等式，并运用零容量论证处理$W^+$的零点集和非紧情形下的无穷远点。

Result: 1) 紧致爱因斯坦四流形上，$W^+$最大特征值处处单重时，至多二重覆盖下度量共形于正数量曲率Kähler度量；2) 该结论推广到调和自对偶Weyl曲率情形；3) 对一致单重假设的爱因斯坦度量，要么$W^+\equiv 0$，要么$W^+$处处非零且共形Kähler；4) 得到完备Ricci平坦四流形的推广及Kähler-Einstein曲面全纯截面曲率的最优夹挤定理。

Conclusion: 自对偶Weyl曲率最大特征值的单重性在爱因斯坦四流形上强烈约束几何结构，导致共形Kähler性；所得夹挤定理是最优的，K3曲面和多中心Gibbons-Hawking瞬子表明假设不可放松。

Abstract: For a compact, connected, oriented Einstein four-manifold, we prove that, if the largest eigenvalue of the self-dual Weyl curvature $W^+$ is everywhere simple, then, after at worst passing to a double cover, the metric is conformally Kähler with positive scalar curvature; more generally, this result holds for metrics with harmonic self-dual Weyl curvature. For Einstein metrics satisfying a uniform simplicity hypothesis on the largest eigenvalue, we further prove that either $W^+\equiv 0$, or $W^+$ nowhere vanishes and the previous conclusion holds. We also obtain extensions to complete Ricci-flat four-manifolds and an optimal pinching theorem for the holomorphic sectional curvature of compact Kähler--Einstein surfaces. The proof combines LeBrun's conformal normalization with the resulting weighted divergence equation and new first-order identities. A zero-capacity argument allows this method to be used across the zero set of $W^+$ and at infinity in the noncompact case. Finally, K3 surfaces and multicentered Gibbons--Hawking gravitational instantons show that our assumptions are sharp.

</details>
