### [143] [Unveiling the Vanishing Higgsino-Nucleon Scattering in the MSSM at Next-to-Leading Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20588)
*Subhadip Bisal,Arindam Chatterjee,Debottam Das,Syed Adil Pasha,Rahul Puri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Higgsino暗物质在电弱自然性要求下的性质，发现NLO辐射修正会显著降低DM-核子散射截面至中微子地板以下，并详细分析了造成散射抑制的抵消机制以及MSSM参数空间中自旋无关截面可能消失的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱自然性要求Higgsino质量接近弱标度，但纯Higgsino态通常能避开当前的直接探测限制。然而，NLO阶辐射修正可能使其进入未来实验的灵敏度范围，因此需要精确研究其散射行为。

Method: 通过计算NLO阶辐射修正，特别是在两个粒子产生的运动学阈值附近，分析DM-核子散射截面的行为；检查抵消机制并识别MSSM参数空间中自旋无关截面消失的区域。

Result: NLO修正将DM-核子散射截面降低至中微子地板以下；发现了导致Higgsino-核子散射被抑制的抵消机制；识别了MSSM参数空间中自旋无关截面可能为零的区域。

Conclusion: 尽管NLO修正可能使Higgsino进入未来实验灵敏度范围，但在阈值附近其散射截面反而低于中微子地板，这使得直接探测更具挑战性，但也为参数空间中的零点识别提供了新的理论方向。

Abstract: Higgsino dark matter (DM) is considered one of the most well-motivated and minimal DM scenarios arising from supersymmetric extensions of the Standard Model. Motivated by the requirement of electroweak naturalness, Higgsinos are expected to be relatively light, with masses close to the weak scale. While a pure Higgsino state typically evades current direct detection limits, next-to-leading (NLO) order radiative corrections may bring it within the sensitivity of upcoming experiments. On the contrary, a more important consequence, observed specifically near the kinematic threshold for the production of two particles, is that the NLO corrections lower the DM-nucleon cross section below the neutrino floor. We explicitly examine the cancellation mechanism responsible for suppressed Higgsino-nucleon scattering and identify regions of MSSM parameter space where spin-independent cross-sections may vanish.

</details>


### [144] [Accidentally Stable Dark Matter in a Parity Solution to the Strong CP Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20600)
*Matthew J. Baldwin,Keisuke Harigaya,Isaac R. Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出具有宇称对称性和扩展规范群 $SU(3)_c \times SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R \times U(1)_X$ 的模型，通过标准模型希格斯粒子的宇称伙伴破缺 $SU(2)_R\times U(1)_X$ 来解决强CP问题，并研究了该框架中偶然稳定的暗物质——$SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R$ 双重态-三重态费米子。计算了其 relic丰度，并从对撞机、直接探测和间接探测实验导出参数空间约束，要求 $SU(2)_R\times U(1)_X$ 破缺标度低于150 TeV，且大部分参数空间可被近期的间接探测实验探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强CP问题，并在该框架中探索偶然稳定的暗物质候选者。

Method: 利用宇称对称性和扩展规范群 $SU(3)_c \times SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R \times U(1)_X$，通过标准模型希格斯粒子的宇称伙伴破缺 $SU(2)_R\times U(1)_X$ 来解决强CP问题；引入 $SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R$ 双重态-三重态费米子作为暗物质候选者，计算其 relic丰度，并从对撞机、直接探测和间接探测实验进行约束分析。

Result: $SU(2)_R\times U(1)_X$ 破缺标度需低于150 TeV；大部分参数空间可被近期的间接探测实验探测到。

Conclusion: 该模型可同时解决强CP问题和提供偶然稳定的暗物质候选者，且未来实验有望探测到大部分参数空间。

Abstract: Parity symmetry, with an extended gauge group $SU(3)_c \times SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R \times U(1)_X$, can solve the strong CP problem. In particular, the model where $SU(2)_R\times U(1)_X$ is broken by the Parity partner of the Standard Model Higgs solves the strong CP problem without the necessity of introducing extra symmetry. We discuss the possibility of accidentally stable dark matter in this framework and show that $SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R$ bi-triplet fermions can be stable over cosmological timescales. We compute the relic abundance of the bi-triplet dark matter and derive constraints on the parameter space from collider, direct-detection, and indirect-detection experiments. The $SU(2)_R\times U(1)_X$ symmetry breaking scale is required to be below 150 TeV, and most of the parameter space can be probed by near-future indirect-detection experiments.

</details>


### [145] [Earth rotation turns event timing into a geometric probe of UHE neutrino origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20608)
*Andrew Cheek,João Paulo Pinheiro,Jordi Salvado*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对KM3NeT探测到的极端高能事件KM3-230213A，提出了一种基于单事件的统计检验方法，评估其由暗物质衰变解释的可能性。研究发现，虽然暗物质衰变假说未被排除（p值约0.13-0.15），但通过时间分辨分析可显著减少排除该假说所需的事件数（从~33-43降至~14-16），表明事件时序信息对区分信号几何结构具有重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KM3NeT探测到的极端高能事件KM3-230213A的到达方向与银河系中心相反，这使得暗物质衰变解释看似不太可能。作者旨在量化评估这一可能性，并发展一种统计方法，利用事件时序信息来区分暗物质衰变信号与各向同性背景信号。

Method: 作者开发了基于单事件的检验统计量，通过p值量化暗物质衰变假说的可能性；引入时间平均探测器可见性分析，并进一步发展为全时间分辨预测方法；比较了不同暗物质衰变通道和晕分布下的结果。

Result: 单事件观测下暗物质衰变假说虽然不利但未被排除（p值约0.13-0.15）；时间平均可见性分析将所需排除事件数从~33-43降至~22-27；全时间分辨预测进一步将所需事件数减少40%，降至~14-16。

Conclusion: 事件时序信息提供的关键信息使得用更少的事件就能排除或确认暗物质假说；该框架可将事件时序转化为信号几何结构的探针，适用于赤道中微子望远镜探测的极端高能事件。

Abstract: It has been proposed that the ultra-high-energy (UHE) event $\rm KM3-230213A$ detected by KM3NeT could be explained by dark matter (DM) decay. Prima facie this seems unlikely because the arrival direction of the event is opposite to the Galactic Centre. We develop a per-event test statistic to quantitively assess this possibility and forecast the required future events to exclude the DM hypothesis in favour of an isotropic signal. For the single event observed, the DM decay hypothesis is disfavoured but not excluded ($p\text{-value}_{\rm DM}\simeq0.13$--$0.15$). We emphasise how including the time-averaged detector visibility helps discrimination despite reducing the proportion of visible sky, reducing the number of events for exclusion from $\sim33$--$43$ to $\sim22$--$27$. Moving beyond this, we perform a fully time-resolved forecast and find that the required number of events for exclusion reduces by $40\%$, $\sim14$--$16$. The time variation in the signal provides vital information allowing one to exclude or confirm the DM hypothesis with much fewer events. Our results are robust against DM decay channels and halo distributions and can readily be applied to other relics distributed similarly. Our framework allows one to turn event timing into a probe of signal geometry and is generic to any UHE equatorial neutrino telescopes.

</details>


### [146] [Dark Matter Weather: Probing Sub-GeV Interactions with Earth-Shielding Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20620)
*Tetiana Kozynets,Rebecca Leane,Juri Smirnov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用地球屏蔽导致的每日调制效应，研究亚GeV暗物质与电子和原子核的双重相互作用机制，并开发了一种结合调制形状与电离谱的统计分析方法，在液氩/氙探测器及DarkSide-50数据上展示了区分电子与核相互作用的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚GeV暗物质探测中，低阈值实验的地球屏蔽每日调制效应提供了有力的搜索手段，但现有方法未充分利用这一效应对相互作用结构的甄别能力。本文旨在通过双重相互作用框架（核散射改变入射通量，电子散射产生可观测信号）来解耦暗物质与电子和原子核的耦合特性。

Method: 针对液氩和液氙靶，建立双重相互作用框架：地球中的核散射调制入射暗物质通量，探测器中的电子散射产生电离信号。引入新的统计分析，同时检验调制形状（包括不同地下实验室位置依赖的地球穿越轨迹曝光）和电离能谱，并将两者结合分析。在SURF（美国）、LNGS（意大利）、SUPL（澳大利亚）三个地下站点及DarkSide-50数据上进行案例研究。

Result: 地球散射调制效应能够帮助区分暗物质与电子和原子核的相互作用类型，可为低阈值液氩/液氙探测提供有效的验证手段。

Conclusion: 双重相互作用框架下的调制形状分析是一种有前景的工具，可用于甄别暗物质相互作用结构，并为未来低阈值液氩/液氙实验提供交叉验证。

Abstract: Daily modulation from Earth shielding provides a powerful search handle for sub-GeV dark matter (DM) in low-threshold experiments. We use this effect as a probe of interaction structure in scenarios where DM couples to both electrons and nuclei. Nuclear scattering in the Earth alters the incident DM flux, while electron scattering produces the observable ionization signal, so the total rate and modulation pattern encode different aspects of the underlying interactions. We develop this two-interaction framework for argon and xenon targets and introduce a new statistical analysis that tests the modulation shape, including the location-dependent exposure of underground detectors to different Earth-crossing trajectories, alone and in combination with the ionization spectrum. We demonstrate the method for liquid-noble detectors at underground sites SURF (USA), LNGS (Italy), and SUPL (Australia), and apply it to DarkSide-50 data as a concrete case study. Our results show that Earth-scattering modulation can help disentangle electron and nuclear DM interactions and provide a validation handle for low-threshold liquid-noble searches.

</details>


### [147] [Reheating the FCC: Probing Early Matter Domination with Long-Lived Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20625)
*Nicolás Bernal,Giovanna Cottin,Kuldeep Deka,Manuel López*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个GeV量级的希格斯门户标量粒子φ，该粒子可以在早期宇宙中主导能量密度，随后衰变为标准模型粒子，结束早期物质主导时代。通常的宇宙再加热是该情景的最小实例。希格斯混合使得φ成为长寿命粒子，将再加热温度T_rh与对撞机上的位移衰变信号联系起来。考虑衰变宽度的有限温度抑制效应，本文将FCC-hh对长寿命粒子的探测灵敏度映射到[m_φ, T_rh]参数平面。研究发现FCC-hh的位移探测能够探测到GeV量级的转变温度，最高可达电弱能标，且该描述在希格斯门户的破缺相有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GeV能标希格斯门户标量粒子作为早期宇宙物质主导阶段的来源，并通过其对撞机信号（位移衰变）与宇宙再加热温度建立联系，寻找新的探测途径。

Method: 理论分析希格斯门户标量粒子的宇宙学演化，考虑有限温度效应对衰变宽度的抑制，将FCC-hh对长寿命粒子的位移探测灵敏度映射到质量-再加热温度参数空间。

Result: FCC-hh的位移探测能够探测GeV量级的转变温度，最高可达电弱能标，且该描述在希格斯门户的破缺相有效。

Conclusion: 希格斯门户标量粒子作为早期宇宙物质主导阶段的候选者，其长寿命特性使得对撞机位移探测成为研究宇宙再加热过程的有效手段，FCC-hh有望探测从GeV到电弱能标的转变温度范围。

Abstract: We study a GeV-scale Higgs-portal scalar $φ$ that can dominate the early Universe and later decay into Standard Model states, ending an early matter-dominated era. Ordinary cosmic reheating is the minimal example of this scenario. The small Higgs mixing makes $φ$ a long-lived particle (LLP), linking the reheating temperature $T_\text{rh}$ to displaced-decay signatures at colliders. Including finite-temperature suppression of the decay width, we map FCC-hh LLP sensitivity onto the $[m_φ, T_\text{rh}]$ plane. We find that FCC-hh displaced searches could probe GeV-scale transition temperatures, up to the electroweak scale within the broken-phase Higgs-portal description.

</details>


### [148] [Hyperasymptotic Expansions in QCD: The Lightest Gluelump Mass Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20648)
*Antonio Pineda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD中超渐近展开方法计算了最轻胶团（gluelump）的质量，通过确定领先重整子归一化常数并从两个独立物理系统中提取胶团质量，最终得到了重整化群不变且与方案无关的结果Λ_B^PV = 2.44(7) r_0^{-1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定最轻胶团的质量，这是QCD非微扰物理中的重要量，对理解夸克禁闭和胶子动力学具有关键意义。

Method: 采用超渐近展开（hyperasymptotic expansions）方法，精确确定领先重整子（renormalon）归一化常数，并从两个独立物理系统中提取胶团质量。

Result: 最终得到组合的、重整化群不变且与方案无关的结果Λ_B^PV = 2.44(7) r_0^{-1}。

Conclusion: 通过超渐近展开方法成功确定了最轻胶团的质量，所得结果具有重整化群不变性和方案无关性，为QCD非微扰研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: In these proceedings, we provide a summary of our recent advancements in determining the mass of the lightest gluelump using hyperasymptotic expansions in Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) \cite{Ayala2025}. This paper details our methodology, our precise determination of leading renormalon normalization constants, and our extraction of the gluelump mass from two independent physical systems, yielding a final combined, renormalization-group-invariant and scheme-independent result of $Λ_{B}^{\rm PV} = 2.44(7) r_0^{-1}$.

</details>


### [149] [Spin Structure of the Nucleon: Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20761)
*Yoshitaka Hatta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对质子自旋分解进行了教学性综述，重点讨论了Jaffe-Manohar和Ji两种分解方案，特别关注夸克和胶子的轨道角动量及其规范不变定义、小x行为以及与实验观测量之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子自旋结构是量子色动力学(QCD)中的一个基本问题，理解夸克和胶子如何贡献质子的自旋对于深入认识强相互作用和核子结构至关重要。

Method: 采用教学性综述方法，系统梳理了Jaffe-Manohar分解和Ji分解两种理论框架，重点分析了夸克与胶子轨道角动量的规范不变定义及其在小x区域的渐近行为。

Result: 阐明了两种分解方案的区别与联系，揭示了轨道角动量的规范不变构造方式，并建立了与实验可观测量（如深非弹性散射、广义部分子分布等）之间的理论关联。

Conclusion: 质子自旋分解仍是一个活跃的研究领域，夸克和胶子轨道角动量的精确测量需要理论与实验的协同推进。

Abstract: I present a pedagogical review of the decomposition of the proton spin. Both the Jaffe-Manohar and Ji decompositions are discussed. Particular emphasis is placed on the quark and gluon orbital angular momenta, including their gauge invariant definitions, small-$x$ behavior and connection to experimental observables.

</details>


### [150] [Numerical Backreaction and Finite-Time Energy Transfer in Post-Recombination Magnetogenesis from Ultralight Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20793)
*Aviv David,Robert Brandenberger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了后复合时代由超轻赝标量暗物质场φ耦合电磁感应产生的规范场。研究发现，在快子不稳定性带开放时，反作用不会阻止共振，直到约初始暗物质能量密度的一小部分转移到光子，且时间尺度远小于哈勃时间；在仅窄共振带开放的参数范围内，转移分数最终可达数量级1，但所需时间随有效耦合常数减小而增加，因此该分数至今可能仍可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证和扩展先前关于后复合时代超轻赝标量暗物质场φ耦合电磁感应产生规范场的解析近似结果，并确定反作用何时会关闭共振过程。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，在膨胀背景中同时追踪均匀振荡的φ模和两种螺旋度的规范场模的耦合演化过程。

Result: 数值结果证实了先前解析近似给出的规范粒子产生谱；发现快子不稳定性带开放时，反作用不会关闭共振直到约初始暗物质能量密度的相当一部分转移到光子；仅窄共振带开放时，转移分数最终可达量级1，但时间随有效耦合常数减小而延长。

Conclusion: 对于快子不稳定性带开放的参数值，反作用不会阻止共振，能量转移高效且快速；对于仅窄共振带开放的参数范围，能量转移效率取决于有效耦合常数，可能至今仍可忽略。

Abstract: We study gauge-field production in a post-recombination magnetogenesis scenario driven by an ultralight pseudoscalar dark matter field $φ$ coupled to electromagnetism. Previous analytical work has shown that a homogeneous oscillating $φ$ background can excite gauge field modes through tachyonic and narrow-band resonance channels. Here we follow the coupled evolution of the homogeneous $φ$ mode and gauge field modes of both helicities in an expanding background. Our numerical work confirms the results for the spectra of produced gauge particles obtained in the previous approximate analytical treatments. In addition, our study allows us to determine when back-reaction shuts off the resonance. We find that for parameter values for which the tachyonic instability band is open, back-reaction does not shut off the resonance until a fraction ${\cal{F}}$ of order one of the initial dark matter energy density has been transferred to photons, and this happens on a time scale which is short compared to the Hubble time. In the parameter range in which only the narrow resonance band is open, ${\cal{F}}$ eventually reaches order one, but the time when this happens increases as the effective coupling constant decreases, and therefore ${\cal{F}}$ may remain negligible until the present time.

</details>


### [151] [The supernumerary $K(1690)$ signal from COMPASS as a strange hybrid state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20825)
*Bing Chen,Ri-Qing Qian,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对COMPASS合作组发现的三个奇异介子态K(1460)、K(1690)和K(1830)进行了系统研究。在夸克模型框架下，1.0-2.0 GeV区域只预期存在2¹S₀和3¹S₀两个奇异介子态，因此至少有一个态是奇特态候选。研究发现K(1460)和K(1830)可分别归类为2¹S₀和3¹S₀奇异介子，强衰变分析支持这一指认。而K(1690)的产生机制与公认的混杂介子候选π₁(1600)相似，在组分胶子模型下将其视为0⁻奇异混杂介子，研究支持其混杂态性质。最后指出了可用于实验检验的K(1690)衰变模式，并建议BESIII通过J/ψ→KK(1690)→KK*₀(1430)π→KKππ过程搜索K(1690)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMPASS实验在1.0-2.0 GeV能量区域发现了三个奇异介子态K(1460)、K(1690)和K(1830)，但夸克模型在该区域只预期有两个态(2¹S₀和3¹S₀)，因此至少有一个态是超越标准夸克模型的奇特态候选，需要对其性质进行系统鉴别。

Method: 1) 系统研究奇异介子谱，对三个态进行夸克模型分类；2) 计算强衰变检验K(1460)和K(1830)作为常规夸克模型的指认；3) 类比K(1690)与π₁(1600)的产生机制；4) 在组分胶子模型框架下将K(1690)作为0⁻奇异混杂介子研究其强衰变。

Result: K(1460)和K(1830)分别是2¹S₀和3¹S₀奇异介子的良好候选，强衰变分析支持该指认。K(1690)的产生机制与混杂介子候选π₁(1600)类似，将其作为0⁻奇异混杂介子研究时，强衰变结果支持其混杂态性质。

Conclusion: K(1460)和K(1830)是常规夸克模型态，而K(1690)更可能是一个奇异混杂介子。本文还提出了可用于实验检验K(1690)性质的衰变模式，并建议BESIII通过J/ψ衰变过程搜索K(1690)。

Abstract: The $K(1690)$ state with $J^P=0^-$ was recently discovered by the COMPASS Collaboration in the scattering reaction $K^-+p\to K^-π^-π^++p$, leading to three observed pseudoscalar strange mesons, namely the $K(1460)$, $K(1690)$, and $K(1830)$, in the 1.0$-$2.0 GeV region. However, within the quark model, only the $2^1S_0$ and $3^1S_0$ strange meson states are expected in this energy region. Therefore, at least one of these states should be interpreted as an exotic candidate. In this work, we study the spectrum of strange mesons systematically, and find that the $K(1460)$ and $K(1830)$ are good candidates of $2^1S_0$ and $3^1S_0$ strange mesons, respectively. A further investigation of their strong decays also supports this assignment. Furthermore, we note that the production mechanism of the $K(1690)$ is similar to that of the $π_1(1600)$, which has been widely regarded as a hybrid meson candidate. We investigate the strong decays of the $K(1690)$ within a constituent gluon model by treating it as a $0^-$ strange hybrid meson and find that the results support the $K(1690)$ as a hybrid state. Finally, we identify several important decay modes of the $K(1690)$ that may be used in future experiments to test whether it contains the $n^1S_0$ ($n=$2 and 3) $s\bar{q}$ component. We also suggest BESIII to search for the $K(1690)$ state through the $J/ψ\to K K(1690) \to KK^{\ast}_0(1430)π\to KKππ$ process.

</details>


### [152] [Dark Monopoles, Bounds on Hidden Sectors, and Cosmological Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20843)
*Donald Liveoak,Anshuman Maharana,James D. Wells*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了隐藏扇区磁单极子在早期宇宙热相变中产生并通过粒子对湮灭稀释的过程，发现对称性破缺标度≥100 PeV时单极子丰度过高会过度闭合宇宙，该界限对隐藏扇区初始能量密度分数变化不敏感，仅在多个隐藏扇区时显著收紧。标准宇宙学只有在隐藏扇区不存在、无高标度单极子、再加热温度低于破缺标度或早期物质主导期稀释单极子等条件下才能恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论紧化普遍预言隐藏扇区的存在，这为暗物质模型构建提供了有前景的方向。本文旨在评估隐藏扇区磁单极子对宇宙演化的影响，特别是其丰度是否会导致宇宙过度闭合。

Method: 考虑隐藏扇区磁单极子通过早期宇宙热相变产生，随后通过粒子对湮灭稀释。通过分析对称性破缺标度与单极子丰度的关系，建立对宇宙闭合程度的约束边界，并考察初始能量密度分数和隐藏扇区数量等参数的影响。

Result: 对称性破缺标度≥100 PeV时，单极子丰度不可接受地过高，导致宇宙过度闭合。该界限对隐藏扇区初始能量密度分数表现出弱的幂律依赖性，但在多个隐藏扇区时界限显著收紧。

Conclusion: 标准宇宙学可恢复的条件包括：隐藏扇区不存在、隐藏扇区无对称性破缺标度高于100 PeV的单极子、各单极子产生扇区再加热后最高温度低于其对称性破缺标度、或早期物质主导期稀释了单极子丰度。

Abstract: Hidden sectors are a generic prediction of string theory compactifications and result in a promising landscape for dark matter model building. We consider the case of hidden sector magnetic monopoles produced via a thermal phase transition in the early Universe and subsequently diluted by pair annihilation. We show that for symmetry-breaking scales $\gtrsim 100\, \text{PeV}$, the monopole abundance is unacceptably high, overclosing the Universe. Our bounds are robust against variations in the initial fraction of energy density deposited in the hidden sector, exhibiting only a weak power-law dependence on this quantity. The bound is substantially tightened in the case of multiple hidden sectors. The standard cosmology may only be recovered if one of the following is true: the hidden sector(s) are non-existent, the hidden sectors have no monopoles with symmetry-breaking scale above 100 PeV, the maximum temperature of each monopole-producing hidden sector after reheating is below its symmetry-breaking scale, or the monopole abundance is diluted during a period of early matter domination.

</details>


### [153] [Charged Higgs Search at Future Neutrino Telescope and Higgs Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20963)
*YiCheng Dai,Wei Liao,Yi-Song Lu,Qi-Shu Yan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电希格斯玻色子在两种简化的普适汤川耦合基准场景下的发现潜力，利用未来轻子对撞机和neutrino望远镜。通过中微子共振散射过程，研究了中微子望远镜中的μ子径迹和级联事件对带电希格斯玻色子的搜索与约束能力，并比较了不同天体物理中微子通量模型下的灵敏度。同时，通过优化重建策略研究了未来轻子对撞机在不同运动学区间对带电希格斯玻色子的信号探测能力。两种实验途径的比较表明，未来轻子对撞机在大部分参数空间提供更好的灵敏度，而具有超大探测器体积的neutrino望远镜在带电希格斯玻色子较重区域可提供有竞争力和互补性的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索带电希格斯玻色子的发现潜力，比较未来轻子对撞机和neutrino望远镜两种实验途径在相关参数空间中的探测能力，寻找互补的搜索策略。

Method: 在两种简化基准场景下，利用中微子望远镜的中微子共振散射（μ子径迹和级联事件）和未来轻子对撞机的优化重建策略，分析不同运动学区间的信号。

Result: 未来轻子对撞机在大部分参数空间提供更好的灵敏度；具有超大探测器体积的neutrino望远镜在带电希格斯玻色子的重质量区域可提供竞争性和互补性的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 两种实验途径具有互补性，未来轻子对撞机是主要探测手段，而大型neutrino望远镜在重质量区域具有独特优势。

Abstract: We investigate the discovery potential of the charged Higgs boson at future lepton colliders and neutrino telescopes within two simplified benchmark scenarios with universal Yukawa couplings. We demonstrate that both the muon-track and cascade events at neutrino telescopes can be exploited to search for or constrain the charged Higgs boson through resonant neutrino scattering process, and we compare the sensitivities obtained under different astrophysical neutrino flux models. We study the effect of the detector volume of neutrino telescopes on the discovery potential of the charged Higgs boson. We study the signal of charged Higgs boson at future lepton colliders over different kinematic regimes using optimized reconstruction strategies. We compare the discovery potential of the two experimental approaches over the relevant parameter space and find that future lepton colliders generally provide better sensitivity in most of the parameter space, while neutrino telescopes with very large detector volume can offer competitive and complementary sensitivity in the heavy-mass region of the charged Higgs boson.

</details>


### [154] [Advanced Statistical Analysis of Linear and Nonlinear Regge Trajectories for Light Non-strange Mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21035)
*S. S. Afonin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对轻非奇异介子Regge轨迹进行了严谨的统计分析，考虑了实验不确定性。测试了三种模型（线性与非线性），通过χ²最小化、信息准则和统计检验进行模型选择。结果表明，Regge谱的非线性主要局域在S波共振区，非线性模型（含类Dirac-Coulomb项）能成功描述这些畸变，并揭示出近似的Coulomb型简并性。l依赖的修正与Coulomb问题的相对论领头阶修正一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轻非奇异介子Regge轨迹分析通常未充分考虑实验不确定性和模型选择问题，对线性假设的偏离也缺乏严格的统计检验和理论解释。

Method: 采用非线性χ²最小化框架，自洽地确定理论模型不确定性（强制χ²/dof=1）；利用Akaike和Bayesian信息准则以及补充统计检验进行模型选择；测试了三种模型：不同斜率线性模型（Model 1）、通用斜率线性模型（Model 2）、含通用斜率和类Dirac-Coulomb项∝(l+1)⁻¹的非线性模型（Model 3）。

Result: Regge谱的适度非线性偏离主要局域在S波共振区；非线性修正（Model 3）成功描述了这些偏离；发现了近似的Coulomb型简并性m²(n,l)∝n+l；l依赖的修正与Coulomb问题的相对论领头阶修正一致。

Conclusion: 非线性Regge轨迹模型（Model 3）是描述轻非奇异介子质量谱的最优选择，其结构在物理上对应于相对论Coulomb问题的领头阶修正，为理解介子内部动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: A rigorous statistical analysis of Regge-type trajectories for light non-strange meson mass spectra is carried out, which explicitly accounts for experimental uncertainties. We test three scenarios: a linear model with distinct radial ($n$) and orbital ($l$) slopes (Model 1), a linear model with a universal slope (Model 2), and a nonlinear model featuring a universal slope and a Dirac-Coulomb-type term $\propto (l+1)^{-1}$ (Model 3). Optimization is performed using a nonlinear $χ^2$ minimization framework, where the intrinsic theoretical model uncertainty is determined self-consistently by enforcing $χ^2/\text{dof} = 1$. To ensure robust model selection, we employ the Akaike and Bayesian information criteria alongside complementary statistical tests. Our analysis demonstrates that moderate deviations from linearity in the Regge spectrum are predominantly localized within the $S$-wave resonance sector. These distortions are successfully accommodated by the nonlinear correction in Model 3, from which an approximate Coulomb-type degeneracy, $m^2(n,l) \propto n+l$, emerges as a statistically robust feature. Furthermore, we show that the $l$-dependent correction to the principal quantum number in light non-strange mesons is consistent with the leading-order relativistic correction to the Coulomb problem.

</details>


### [155] [MeV Electrophilic Axion-like Particles from Sun](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21168)
*Shao-Feng Ge,Sk Jeesun,Tao Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用太阳核聚变产生的5.5MeV单色光子通过类康普顿散射产生MeV级类轴子粒子(ALPs)，并利用现有及未来的暗物质直接探测实验（LZ、PandaX-4T、Borexino、PandaX-xT）对其进行探测，在0.4-1MeV质量窗口内获得了新的参数约束，最高灵敏度可达g_{ae}≲1.5×10^{-7}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用太阳核聚变产生的5.5MeV单色光子作为源，通过类康普顿散射产生MeV级类轴子粒子(ALPs)，以探测此前未被探索的参数空间区域。

Method: 利用太阳核聚变产生的5.5MeV单色光子与太阳物质中的环境电子发生类康普顿散射，产生通量可观的MeV级ALPs；这些ALPs到达地球后通过与电子的相同耦合被暗物质直接探测实验探测到。

Result: LZ和PandaX-4T可达到g_{ae}≲3.7×10^{-6}的灵敏度，Borexino可达g_{ae}≲1.6×10^{-6}；PandaX-xT（200吨·年曝光）在m_a<1MeV范围内可达g_{ae}≲1.6×10^{-6}，在m_a接近1MeV时可达g_{ae}≲1.5×10^{-7}；在0.4MeV≲m_a≲1MeV质量窗口内获得了新的参数约束。

Conclusion: 现有LZ、PandaX-4T和Borexino数据以及未来PandaX-xT实验能够在0.4-1MeV质量窗口内探测到新的参数区域，为MeV级ALPs的探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: This work explores the production of an MeV-scale electrophilic axion-like particles (ALPs) by utilizing the monochromatic 5.5MeV photon resulting from the nuclear fusion processes in the Sun. These 5.5MeV photons can undergo the Compton-like scattering with the ambient electrons in the solar matter to produce a substantial flux of MeV ALPs. Upon reaching the Earth, such ALPs can be detected via the same electron coupling, offering a new opportunity for the dark matter (DM) direct detection experiments to probe the previously unexplored parameter regions. We show that the existing data of LZ, PandaX-4T, and Borexino can attain the sensitivities $g_{ae} \lesssim 3.7 \times 10^{-6}$, $g_{ae} \lesssim 3.7 \times 10^{-6}$ and $g_{ae} \lesssim 1.7 \times 10^{-6}$, respectively, for $m_a \lesssim 1$MeV. An optimistic 200 tonne$\times$year exposure by PandaX-xT can reach $g_{ae}\lesssim 1.6 \times 10^{-6}$ for most of the mass window $m_a < 1$MeV and even $g_{ae} \lesssim 1.5 \times 10^{-7}$ with $m_a$ approaching 1MeV. Despite the stringent constraints from different laboratory experiments and astrophysical observations, our obtained limits from LZ, PandaX-4T, and Borexino can probe new parameter regions, specifically in the mass window $0.4\,{\rm MeV} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 1$MeV.

</details>


### [156] [Thermal Resummation for Very Strong First-order Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21252)
*Philipp Klose*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对强一阶相变中随机引力波背景预测的关键问题——有效势计算，指出传统高温展开方法在强相变（可产生LISA可观测信号）时会失效，并提出使用2PI有效作用量技术进行一致重求和。以阿贝尔-希格斯模型为例，在一般协变规范下计算了强相变的次领头阶有效势，发现结果可通过修改功率计数从Daisy重求势恢复，并满足领头阶Nielsen恒等式，与希格斯凝聚较小时的先前结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高温展开的维度约化等有效势计算方法在强一阶相变（能产生下一代引力波探测器如LISA可观测背景信号）中预期失效，因此需要发展新的、一致的重求和技术来可靠计算此类强相变的有效势。

Method: 采用2PI有效作用量技术进行一致重求和，在一般协变规范下计算了阿贝尔-希格斯模型的次领头阶有效势，并与修改功率计数后的Daisy重求势进行比较，同时验证了领头阶Nielsen恒等式。

Result: 计算得到的次领头阶有效势可从Daisy重求势通过修改功率计数恢复，且满足领头阶Nielsen恒等式，保证了气泡成核率规范无关预测的一致性，并与小希格斯凝聚情况下的先前结果一致。

Conclusion: 2PI有效作用量技术能够为强一阶相变提供一致的重求和方案，克服了传统高温展开方法的局限性，为未来LISA等探测器可观测的引力波背景预测提供了更可靠的理论工具。

Abstract: Effective potentials are a key ingredient for predicting stochastic gravitational wave backgrounds from strong first-order phase transitions in the early universe. Established techniques for a robust computation, including dimensional reduction, rely on a high-temperature expansion that is expected to break down for very strong transitions capable of producing observable backgrounds at next-generation gravitational wave detectors such as LISA. We argue that existing 2PI effective action techniques enable consistent resummation for such transitions, and use them to compute the next-to-leading order effective potential of the Abelian Higgs model for a strong transition in a general covariant gauge. We find that our result can be recovered from a Daisy resummed potential by modifying the power counting, and show explicitly that it satisfies the leading-order Nielsen identity needed for gauge-independent predictions of the bubble nucleation rate and is consistent with prior results for small Higgs condensates.

</details>


### [157] [Transverse-momentum resummation effects on angular coefficients in Z and W boson hadroproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21395)
*Stefano Camarda,Giancarlo Ferrera,Lorenzo Rossi,Gabriele Francesco Sala*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了LHC（ATLAS、LHCb、CMS）和Tevatron（CDF）实验中的Z/W玻色子产生角系数，通过将NNLL阶小横动量重求和与NLO固定阶计算相结合，发现重求和效应在中间q_T区域（20-50 GeV）对部分角系数的数据描述有适度改善，其余情况则未降低与实验的吻合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全面评估横动量重求和效应对Z/W玻色子产生过程中角系数的理论预测影响，检验其是否能在更广运动学范围内改善与实验数据的符合程度。

Method: 将小横动量区域（q_T）的对数增强QCD修正重求和至NNLL精度，与大横动量区域的NLO固定阶计算进行一致组合，对ATLAS、LHCb、CMS和CDF实验数据中的角系数进行理论预测和对比分析。

Result: 重求和效应在中间q_T区域（20-50 GeV）对多个角系数的数据描述带来适度且系统的改善；在其余角系数和运动学区域，重求和并未降低固定阶QCD预言与实验测量的一致性。

Conclusion: 包含重求和效应的理论计算能改善部分角系数的数据描述，且整体上不会破坏固定阶结果与实验的吻合，验证了重求和在角系数预测中的有效性和稳健性。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of the angular coefficients of $Z$ and $W$ boson production at hadron colliders in different kinematical ranges, using data from the ATLAS, LHCb, and CMS Collaborations at the LHC, as well as CDF data at the Tevatron. We provide theoretical predictions obtained by consistently combining the resummation of logarithmically enhanced QCD corrections at small transverse momenta $q_T$ up to next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy (NNLL) with fixed-order calculations at next-to-leading order (NLO), valid at large $q_T$. We quantify the impact of transverse-momentum resummation on the angular coefficients. We find that the inclusion of resummation effects leads to a moderate and systematic improvement in the description of the data in the intermediate $q_T$ region, $q_T \sim 20-50$~GeV, for several angular coefficients, while in the remaining cases it does not degrade the agreement of fixed-order QCD predictions with experimental measurements.

</details>


### [158] [Heavy neutral leptons from light scalar in fixed target and forward search experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21537)
*ShivaSankar K. A.,Souvik Das,Arindam Das,Sanjoy Mandal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在$U(1)_{B-L}$扩展标准模型框架下，通过稀有介子衰变产生的轻标量粒子衰变为长寿命重中性轻子，并评估FPF和SHiP实验对此过程的探测灵敏度，发现它们能显著扩展对轻标量和长寿命重中微子的发现范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子质量的存在强烈支持标准模型的$U(1)_{B-L}$扩展，其中重中性轻子通过自发对称性破缺获得马约拉纳质量，并经由跷跷板机制产生轻中微子质量。探索这一框架中的新粒子信号是重要的研究动机。

Method: 作者考虑了一个场景：负责对称性破缺的单态标量粒子与SM希格斯粒子混合，从而在稀有介子衰变中产生。该轻标量迅速衰变为一对长寿命重中微子，后者通过轻-重中微子混合在探测器内衰变为可见的带电轻子和强子。研究考虑了FPF（在FCC-hh处）和SHiP束流束实验的真实探测器几何、衰变概率和可见分支比，估算了对标量-希格斯混合角（作为标量质量的函数）以及轻-重中微子混合（作为重中微子质量的函数）的投影灵敏度。

Result: FPF和SHiP能够显著扩展对轻标量和长寿命重中微子的发现范围，超越现有实验限制，为探测中微子质量生成和隐藏扇区物理提供强大且互补的手段。

Conclusion: FPF和SHiP实验可作为探索中微子质量起源和隐藏扇区新物理的有效平台，对$U(1)_{B-L}$模型中的标量-希格斯混合和轻-重中微子混合具有高灵敏度。

Abstract: The observation of neutrino masses strongly motivates $U(1)_{B-L}$ extensions of the Standard Model, in which heavy neutral leptons acquire Majorana masses through spontaneous $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry breaking and generate light neutrino masses via the seesaw mechanism. In this framework, the singlet scalar responsible for symmetry breaking mixes with the SM Higgs boson, allowing it to be produced in rare meson decays. We investigate a scenario in which this light scalar promptly decays into a pair of long-lived heavy neutrinos that subsequently decay into visible charged leptons and hadrons through light-heavy neutrino mixing inside the proposed Forward Physics Facility (FPF) at the FCC-hh and the SHiP beam-dump experiment. Taking into account realistic detector geometries, decay probabilities, and visible branching fractions, we estimate the projected sensitivities to the scalar-Higgs mixing angle as a function of the scalar mass and to the light-heavy neutrino mixing as a function of the heavy neutrino mass. We find that FPF and SHiP can significantly extend the discovery reach for both light scalars and long-lived heavy neutrinos beyond existing experimental limits, providing powerful and complementary probes of neutrino-mass generation and hidden-sector physics.

</details>


### [159] [Neural solutions of coupled ghost and gluon Dyson--Schwinger equations in Landau gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21548)
*Rodrigo Carmo Terin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用神经网络表示法求解四维Landau规范Yang-Mills理论中的耦合鬼胶子和胶子Dyson-Schwinger方程，仅从重整化方程残差训练得到与不动点解在百分水平一致的结果，并验证了其稳定性和截断范围内的紫外行为再现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索用神经网络表示法求解Dyson-Schwinger方程的可行性，无需传统数值方法中的网格离散化和迭代求解，仅依赖方程残差训练即可获得精确解。

Method: 采用神经网络参数化鬼胶子和胶子传播子函数，以重整化后的DSE残差作为损失函数进行训练，无需预先标注数据；在不同初始化、网络大小、积分网格和红外边界条件下测试解的稳定性。

Result: 神经解与不动点解在百分误差水平吻合；改变三胶子顶点模型对解的影响远大于神经网络自身的误差；在截断限制内成功复现MiniMOM紫外跑动和胶子Schwinger函数的符号变化。

Conclusion: 神经网络表示法可作为求解DSE的有效替代方案，具有高精度和稳定性，但解的精度仍受截断近似（如三胶子顶点模型）的限制。

Abstract: The coupled ghost and gluon Dyson--Schwinger equations (DSEs) of four-dimensional Landau-gauge Yang--Mills (YM) theory are solved with a neural representation trained only from renormalized equation residuals. The neural and fixed-point solutions agree at the percent level and remain stable under changes of initialization, network size, integration grid, and infrared boundary condition. Variations of the three-gluon vertex model produce substantially larger effects than the neural error. The MiniMOM ultraviolet running and the sign change of the gluon Schwinger function are also reproduced within the limitations of the truncation.

</details>


### [160] [Gauge coupling beta functions and gauge field anomalous dimensions at four loops in the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21586)
*M. A. Bezuglov,B. A. Kniehl,V. N. Velizhanin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型未破缺相中，计算了规范耦合的四圈贝塔函数和规范场的反常维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准模型中，更高阶的辐射修正确保了理论预测的精确性。计算规范耦合的四圈贝塔函数对于理解渐近自由、大统一理论以及电弱相变等关键物理过程至关重要。

Method: 采用维数正规化和修正的最小减除方案，通过计算四圈级别的费曼图，推导出未破缺相标准模型中规范耦合的重整化群函数。

Result: 得到了规范耦合的四圈贝塔函数以及规范场反常维度的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该结果将标准模型的重整化群函数计算推进到四圈精度，可用于更精确的高能标外推和唯像学分析。

Abstract: We present the beta functions of the gauge couplings and the anomalous dimensions of the gauge fields in the unbroken phase of the Standard Model at four loops.

</details>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [The rank-five Peterson hit problem, the fifth Singer transfer, and a geometric generator in unoriented cobordism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20566)
*Dang Vo Phuc*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了秩五Peterson hit问题在一般族$N_d = 27\cdot 2^d - 5$中的余hit模结构。在度49处通过精确稀疏消去处理292825个单项式，得到hit秩289969和余hit维数2856，确定了权重和分量，并证明Kameko算子的核恰为维数1891的(3,3,2,2,1)权重和分量。计算表明度49处一般线性群不变量构成一维空间，由283项多项式生成，且第五Singer上同调转移在此族中为同构。几何上，证明了Milnor超曲面$H_{2,48}$表示唯一非零不可分解类，但该几何生成元无法与代数不变量的泛函对偶等同，建立了Steenrod理论不变直线与几何配边生成元之间的精确边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peterson hit问题旨在寻找多项式代数$P_s$作为mod-2 Steenrod代数$\mathcal{A}$的不稳定模的最小生成元集。对于秩五的情况，一般容许基失效，Kameko周期性与模不变量的相互作用计算复杂，需要发展新的计算方法来精确描述hit模结构。

Method: 采用精确稀疏消去方法处理度49处的292825个单项式，计算hit秩和余hit维数；利用权重和分解分析Kameko算子的核结构；通过精确不变量计算确定一般线性群不变量的生成元；利用Milnor超曲面的切Stiefel-Whitney数计算，证明其表示唯一非零不可分解类。

Result: 度49处hit秩为289969，余hit维数为2856；确定了确切的权重和分量，其中(3,3,2,2,1)权重和分量为Kameko算子的核，维数1891；一般线性群不变量形成一维空间，由283项多项式生成；第五Singer转移在此族中为同构；度49配边群维数为5692；Milnor超曲面$H_{2,48}$表示唯一非零不可分解类，但不可与代数不变量泛函对偶等同。

Conclusion: 系统纠正了Nguyen Khac Tin先前论文中关于秩五Kameko核和维数的断言；揭示了Steenrod理论不变直线与几何配边生成元之间存在精确边界，Milnor超曲面$H_{2,48}$的几何类与代数不变量的泛函对偶不等同，表明hit问题与几何构造之间存在非平凡对应关系。

Abstract: The Peterson hit problem seeks a minimal set of generators for the polynomial algebra $P_s = \mathbb{F}_2[x_1,\dots,x_s]$ as an unstable module over the mod-2 Steenrod algebra $\mathcal{A}$. For rank five, general admissible bases fail, and the interplay between Kameko periodicity and modular invariants becomes computationally complex. In this paper, we study the rank-five cohit module in the generic family $N_d = 27\cdot 2^d - 5$. Exact sparse elimination in degree $49$ processes $292825$ monomials, yielding a hit rank of $289969$ and a cohit dimension of $2856$. We determine the exact weight summands and prove that the weight-$(3,3,2,2,1)$ summand is exactly the kernel of Kameko's operation, with dimension $1891$. These exact values systematically correct the corresponding rank-five kernel and dimension assertions in Nguyen Khac Tin's previous paper.
  An exact invariant calculation shows that the general linear group invariants in degree $49$ form a one-dimensional space generated by a $283$-term polynomial, and we prove that the fifth Singer cohomological transfer is an isomorphism in this family. Geometrically, the Hilbert-Poincare series of the unoriented cobordism ring gives the dimension of the degree-$49$ cobordism group as $5692$. We prove that the Milnor hypersurface $H_{2,48} \subset \mathbb{R}P^2 \times \mathbb{R}P^{48}$ represents the unique nonzero indecomposable class by computing a tangential Stiefel-Whitney number, providing an explicit geometric generator. However, the evident map from $H_{2,48}$ to the classifying space $B(\mathbb{Z}/2)^5$ sends its fundamental class to a homology class with nonzero $Sq^2_*$. Consequently, this geometric generator cannot be identified with the functional dual of the algebraic invariant, establishing a precise boundary between the Steenrod-theoretic invariant line and the geometric cobordism generator.

</details>


### [162] [Maximal topological complexity of monotone symplectic 4-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20886)
*Ryuma Orita*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了单调辛流形的Farber拓扑复杂度。首先证明了满足某些条件的闭球面单调辛流形自动是环面单调的，进而得到 Kodaira 维数非负且基本群不含 $\ZZ\oplus\ZZ$ 的4维闭球面单调辛流形具有最大拓扑复杂度 $\TC(M)=9$。其次计算了亏格 $g\geq 2$ 的可定向闭曲面上的 $S^2$ 丛的所有 blowup 的拓扑复杂度和Lusternik–Schnirelmann范畴，得到 $\cat(M)=4$，$\TC(M)=7$。最后指出闭辛4-流形在三种情形下实现了 $(\cat,\TC)=(3,5),(4,7),(5,9)$ 的对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续 Or25 的工作，进一步研究单调辛流形的拓扑复杂度，特别是填补其中留下的 $\TC(M)\in\{8,9\}$ 二分法问题，并探讨 Kodaira 维数假设的必要性。

Method: 利用球面单调性与环面单调性之间的关系，结合基本群条件（不含 $\ZZ\oplus\ZZ$ 子群）和 Kodaira 维数分类，对4维情形进行刻画；通过显式计算 blowup 的 $\cat$ 和 $\TC$ 来验证假设的不可缺少性。

Result: (1) 闭球面单调辛流形若基本群不含 $\ZZ\oplus\ZZ$ 子群，则自动是环面单调的。(2) 4维闭球面单调辛流形若 Kodaira 维数非 $-\infty$ 且基本群不含 $\ZZ\oplus\ZZ$（如 Gromov 双曲），则 $\TC(M)=9$。(3) 亏格 $g\geq 2$ 曲面上 $S^2$ 丛的 blowup 满足 $\cat=4,\TC=7$，表明 Kodaira 维数假设不可去除。(4) 闭辛4-流形实现 $(\cat,\TC)=(3,5),(4,7),(5,9)$ 三种配对。

Conclusion: 在比 Or25 更弱的条件下解决了 $\TC(M)\in\{8,9\}$ 的二分法问题，并证明了 Kodaira 维数条件的关键作用，给出了闭辛4-流形拓扑复杂度与Lusternik–Schnirelmann范畴的完整分类。

Abstract: We continue the study of Farber's topological complexity for monotone symplectic manifolds initiated in \cite{Or25}. First, we show that a closed spherically monotone symplectic manifold whose fundamental group contains no subgroup isomorphic to $\ZZ\oplus\ZZ$ is automatically toroidally monotone, with the same monotonicity constant. As a consequence, every closed $4$-dimensional spherically monotone symplectic manifold whose Kodaira dimension is not $-\infty$ and whose fundamental group contains no $\ZZ\oplus\ZZ$ (for instance, is Gromov hyperbolic) has maximal topological complexity $\TC(M)=9$. This settles, under strictly weaker hypotheses, the dichotomy $\TC(M)\in\{8,9\}$ left open there. Second, we compute the topological complexity and the Lusternik--Schnirelmann category of all blowups of $S^2$-bundles over closed orientable surfaces of genus $g\geq 2$: they satisfy $\cat(M)=4$ and $\TC(M)=7$. In particular, the hypothesis on the Kodaira dimension in the first result cannot be removed, and closed symplectic $4$-manifolds realize the pairs $(\cat(M),\TC(M))=(3,5)$, $(4,7)$, $(5,9)$ in the three regimes considered in this paper. Throughout, $\TC$ and $\cat$ are taken in the unreduced convention.

</details>


### [163] [Mixed Hodge formality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21176)
*Pedro Magalhães*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了混合霍奇形式性的概念，它细化了经典形式性并考虑了复代数簇上同调中存在的混合霍奇结构。文章发展了混合霍奇形式性的障碍理论，见证了混合霍奇结构扩张的非平凡性，从而有助于理解某些紧致凯勒流形在混合霍奇意义下的非形式性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典形式性概念未考虑复代数簇上同调中自然的混合霍奇结构，作者希望引入更精细的形式性概念来捕捉这些额外的结构信息，从而更深入地理解某些紧致凯勒流形的非形式性现象。

Method: 定义了混合霍奇形式性的概念，并发展了一套障碍理论，通过混合霍奇结构扩张的非平凡性来刻画非形式性。

Result: 建立了混合霍奇形式性的障碍理论，能够检测混合霍奇结构扩张的非平凡性，并成功解释了某些紧致凯勒流形在混合霍奇意义下的非形式性。

Conclusion: 混合霍奇形式性是对经典形式性的重要细化，为理解复代数几何中微分分次代数的形式性提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of mixed Hodge formality, which refines classical formality and takes into account the mixed Hodge structures present on the cohomology of complex algebraic varieties. We develop an obstruction theory for mixed Hodge formality, witnessing the non-triviality of extensions of mixed Hodge structures. This allows us to understand the non-formality of certain compact Kähler manifolds in the mixed Hodge sense.

</details>


### [164] [Discrete version of topological complexity of maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21330)
*Sutirtha Datta,Navnath Daundkar,Abhishek Sarkar,Soumen Sarkar*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了映射的Scott拓扑复杂度和Murillo-Wu拓扑复杂度的离散类比，证明它们是邻接不变量且彼此等价，并建立了单纯映射的离散拓扑复杂度的基本理论性质和计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将拓扑复杂度的概念从连续映射推广到离散单纯映射，为计算拓扑在离散空间中的应用提供理论基础。

Method: 定义离散版本的Scott和Murillo-Wu拓扑复杂度，通过邻接（contiguity）等价关系证明它们的不变性和等价性，并建立其理论性质与计算方法。

Result: 离散Scott复杂度与离散Murillo-Wu复杂度是邻接不变量，并且两者等价。同时给出了离散拓扑复杂度的基本性质和计算方面结果。

Conclusion: 离散拓扑复杂度为单纯映射提供了一种有效的、与连续情形对应的不变量，具有良好的理论结构并可计算。

Abstract: We introduce and study discrete analogs of Scott's and Murillo-Wu's topological complexity of maps. We prove that these discrete analogs are contiguity invariants and are, in fact, equivalent. Furthermore, we establish the fundamental theoretical properties and computational aspects of the discrete topological complexity of simplicial maps.

</details>


### [165] [On the integral algebraic K-theory of Morava K-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21567)
*Gabriel Angelini-Knoll,Haldun Özgür Bayındır*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了连通Morava K-理论$k(n)$的积分代数K-理论群的基数（在非$0$或$1$模$2p-2$的度数上），并将系数基变换到$\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$后确定了所有度数的基数（偶度为零）。方法上引入了拓扑循环同调上的轨道滤子（源于拓扑Hochschild同调上的May滤子），结合形式DGA $\mathbb{F}[x_{2m}]$的拓扑循环同调分析，得到了具有同伦$\mathbb{F}[x_{2m}]$的$\mathbb{E}_1$-环的强基数结果。对于有限域上的Morava K-理论，进一步使用了Hahn--Raksit--Wilson的动机滤子。作为应用，计算了截断$W(\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p)/p^n$的代数K-理论群的基数（偶度为零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算连通Morava K-理论$k(n)$的积分代数K-理论群的基数，并推广到更一般的$\mathbb{E}_1$-环的拓扑循环同调基数估计，以及截断Witt向量环的代数K-理论群计算。

Method: 引入拓扑循环同调上的轨道滤子（源于May滤子），结合形式DGA $\mathbb{F}[x_{2m}]$的拓扑循环同调分析，以及Hahn--Raksit--Wilson的动机滤子。

Result: 确定了$k(n)$积分代数K-理论群在非$0$或$1$模$2p-2$度数上的基数；基变换到$\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$后确定了所有度数上的基数（偶度为零）。作为应用，计算了$W(\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p)/p^n$的代数K-理论群基数（偶度为零）。

Conclusion: 建立了通过轨道滤子分析拓扑循环同调的有效方法，并给出了多个重要环的代数K-理论群基数的完整描述。

Abstract: We compute the cardinalities of the integral algebraic K-theory groups of connective Morava K-theory $k(n)$ in all degrees that are not congruent to $0$ or $1$ modulo $2p-2$. After base-changing the coefficients of $k(n)$ to the algebraic closure $\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$, we determine the corresponding cardinalities in all degrees and show that the groups vanish in even degrees. Our approach uses what we call the orbit filtration on topological cyclic homology arising from the May filtration on topological Hochschild homology. Combining this with an analysis of the topological cyclic homology of formal DGAs of the form $\mathbb{F}[x_{2m}]$, where $\mathbb{F}$ is a perfect field of characteristic $p$, we prove strong cardinality results for the topological cyclic homology of $\mathbb{E}_1$-rings with homotopy $\mathbb{F}[x_{2m}]$. For Morava K-theories over finite fields, we further use the motivic filtration of Hahn--Raksit--Wilson. As an application of our methods, we compute the cardinalities of the algebraic K-theory groups of the truncation $W(\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p)/p^n$ of the $p$-typical Witt vectors of $\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$; in particular, they vanish in even degrees.

</details>
